### [1] [OmniTable: A Unified Wide-Table System for Petabyte-Scale LLM Data Curation and Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11148)
*Yuzhuo Fu,Xiangchun Wang,Chao Huang,Liyi Wang,Binwei Zeng,Yuhan Wang,Taotao Nie,Dongke Hu,Wang Hong,Jiayi Wang,Wenwen Cui,Zhuyan Zhou,Yushun Guo,Yuhan Xing,Jiaxin Lian,Peng Lin,Qing Cui,Wenhui Shi,Jun Zhou*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: OmniTable 提出一种基于"逻辑统一、物理分离"的统一宽表层架构蓝图，面向PB级LLM数据治理与探索，在生产中管理超35PB训练数据，将人工治理周期从约14天缩短至约2.5天（5.6倍提升）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级LLM开发中，PB级非结构化语料分散在数百张物理表，特征工程依赖人工、以表为中心的流水线编排，且数据血缘基本缺失，数据治理成为关键瓶颈。

Method: OmniTable 包含四项贡献：(1) 统一宽表抽象，通过逻辑-物理映射整合多源异构数据与数千派生特征；(2) 声明式特征生命周期管理，自动完成依赖解析、执行规划、算子融合与血缘追踪，以"声明-执行"范式替代人工流水线编排；(3) 带自治治理的自适应执行引擎，通过CPU/GPU异构计算路由、自适应调优、UDF级容错与自动存储布局优化实现稳定的PB级特征回填；(4) 混合加速数据探索，结合全局ID索引、透明OLAP卸载与后台物化视图，实现秒级点查与超20 TB/小时的过滤导出。

Result: 生产中 OmniTable 管理跨 web、代码、PDF、SFT 等领域的超35PB训练数据，将人工介入的治理周期从约14天降至约2.5天（较此前生产流程5.6倍提升），并保持一致的特性版本管理、可审计血缘与极少人工干预。

Conclusion: OmniTable 以统一宽表层架构有效缓解PB级LLM数据治理瓶颈，兼顾效率、一致性与可审计性，已具备大规模生产可行性。

Abstract: Data curation is a critical bottleneck in industrial-grade LLM development, where petabyte-scale unstructured corpora are scattered across hundreds of physical tables, feature engineering relies on manual, table-centric pipeline orchestration, and data lineage is largely absent. We present OmniTable as an architecture blueprint for a unified wide-table layer built on Logical Unification, Physical Separation, targeting petabyte-scale LLM data curation and exploration. OmniTable makes four contributions: (1) a unified wide-table abstraction that consolidates multi-source heterogeneous data and thousands of derived features under a single logical schema via logical-physical mapping; (2) declarative feature lifecycle management that automates dependency resolution, execution planning, operator fusion, and lineage tracking, replacing manual pipeline orchestration with a "declare-and-execute" paradigm; (3) an adaptive execution engine with autonomous governance that achieves stable PB-scale feature backfill through heterogeneous compute routing (CPU/GPU), adaptive tuning, UDF-level fault tolerance, and automated storage layout optimization; and (4) hybrid-accelerated data exploration combining a global ID index, transparent OLAP offloading, and background materialized views to deliver second-level point lookups and filtered exports exceeding 20 TB/hour. In production, OmniTable manages over 35 PB of training data across web, code, PDF, and SFT domains, reducing the human-in-the-loop curation cycle from approximately 14 days to approximately 2.5 days (5.6x over the pre-OmniTable production workflow), with consistent feature versioning, auditable lineage, and minimal manual intervention.

</details>

### [2] [The Internet of Collaborating Things: Agentic Edge AI for Autonomous Cross-Domain Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10542)
*Walid A. Hanafy,Nader Sehatbakhsh,David Irwin,Mani Srivastava,Prashant Shenoy*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"协作物联网（IoCT）"新范式，主张设备间直接横向协作而非纵向卸载至云端，并探讨agentic边缘AI在其中的作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: IoT设备正迈向万亿美元级规模，且已从简单传感执行端点演变为具备嵌入式处理与智能服务的移动平台。主流的"卸载至云/边缘服务器"纵向交互模型无法随该趋势扩展，因此需要转向设备间直接通信与协作的横向范式。

Method: 作为立场论文，本文定义了IoCT这一新的通信、计算与协作范式，阐述agentic边缘AI在实现该范式中的作用，并勾勒通向可信、自主IoCT协作的研究挑战与未来方向；实现该愿景需要可移植且安全的多异构硬件执行基座，以及具备情境推理、瞬态信任建立和开放世界适应能力的agentic控制面。

Result: 本文为立场论文，未提供实验或量化结果，主要通过概念性论证据论证IoCT范式的必要性与可行性。

Conclusion: 设备应横向直接通信协作，将其计算、感知与执行能力汇聚为动态、跨域集群，无需人工干预即可完成协作全生命周期中的情境推理、瞬态信任与开放世界适应，最终实现可信、自主的IoCT协作。

Abstract: The Internet of Things is on a trajectory toward a trillion connected devices deployed across multiple domains. These devices are no longer simple sensing and actuation endpoints; they are mobile platforms with embedded processing and intelligent on-device services. The dominant paradigm of offloading computation to cloud and edge servers is a vertical device-to-server interaction model that cannot scale with this trajectory. What is needed instead is a horizontal paradigm in which devices communicate and collaborate directly, pooling their compute, sensing, and actuation into dynamic, cross-domain clusters rather than offloading to centralized infrastructure. We refer to this paradigm as the Internet of Collaborating Things (IoCT). Realizing this vision requires both a portable and secure execution substrate for heterogeneous hardware and an agentic control plane capable of contextual reasoning, transient trust establishment, and open-world adaptation throughout the device collaboration lifecycle without human intervention. In this position paper, we define this new communication, compute, and collaboration paradigm, articulate the role of agentic edge AI in realizing it, and outline research challenges and future directions toward trustworthy, autonomous IoCT collaboration.

</details>


### [3] [MUC-FL: Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution for Communication-Efficient Federated Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10545)
*Akshay Mhatre,Vikram Karthick,Deepti Gupta,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution (MUC)框架，按数据块对模型性能的贡献选择性传输，仅需传输1.76%的候选块，可减少45-50%通信量并提升模型质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习虽能实现分布式训练而无需集中数据，但通信开销很高，需要减少传输量同时保持或提升模型性能。

Method: 提出Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution (MUC)框架，基于数据块对模型性能的边际贡献，仅选择性传输影响最大的数据块，并采用基于去重的块选择方法。在整合多个MIMIC临床数据集的多模态数据集上进行评估。

Result: 1135个候选块中仅24个（1.76%）携带有效改进信号，可减少45-50%通信量且保持或提升模型质量；去重块选择的宏F1为0.8566，优于标准联邦优化的0.8155，在欠代表类别上提升尤为明显。

Conclusion: 选择性传输数据块可在显著降低通信开销的同时提升模型性能，尤其改善欠代表类别的表现，为联邦学习通信效率问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Federated Learning (FL) enables distributed model training without centralizing data but suffers from high communication overhead. To address this, we propose Block-Wise Marginal Utility Contribution (MUC), a framework that selectively transmits only the most impactful data blocks based on their contribution to model performance. To evaluate our framework, we apply it to a multimodal dataset integrated from multiple MIMIC clinical datasets and show that only 24 out of 1,135 candidate blocks (1.76%) carry meaningful improvement signals, enabling a potential communication reduction of 45-50% while maintaining or improving model quality. Our deduplication-based block selection achieves a macro F1 score of 0.8566 compared to 0.8155 for standard federated optimization, demonstrating that selective transmission can improve performance, particularly in underrepresented classes.

</details>


### [4] [From Token Interfaces to Token Semantics: A Formal Composition and Conformance Model for Implementation-Neutral Token Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10547)
*John deVadoss*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出以 InterWork Alliance Token Taxonomy Framework (TTF) 作为与实现无关的代币语义规范模型，形式化其构件、公式、组合与分层一致性，并通过案例研究证明代币语义可独立于底层平台进行规范、比较、验证与治理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代币标准（如 ERC-20、ERC-721、ERC-1155）只标准化可调用接口，但未充分规定代币的语义含义。同一 transfer 函数可能隐含不同的供应、可分割性、赎回、取消、证据、治理与生命周期终结假设；而语义等价的代币又可能实现于不同账本和交易模型。这种接口标准与语义规范的脱节，阻碍了跨平台的代币互操作性。

Method: 作者将 TTF 构建为一种类型化语义组合模型，形式化了 TTF 构件（artifacts）、代币公式（token formulas）、行为与属性集组合、良构性约束，以及覆盖公式、构件、消息、状态和轨迹五个层次的符合性（conformance）模型。同时定义了参考验证流程，并说明平台中立的控制消息如何引发符合性义务与实现测试。

Result: 通过文档代币、仓单以及碳/数字 MRV 三个案例研究，并结合接口标准本身无法暴露的代表性无效组合，对模型进行了分析性评估。结果表明，代币语义可独立于平台绑定进行规范、比较、验证、映射与治理。

Conclusion: TTF 为跨智能合约平台、许可账本和共享状态系统提供了更可靠的代币互操作基础，使代币语义规范能够与具体实现解耦。

Abstract: Digital-token standards such as ERC-20, ERC-721, and ERC-1155 have been essential to blockchain adoption because they standardize callable interfaces. Interface standardization, however, does not fully specify token meaning. Two implementations may expose the same transfer function while encoding different assumptions about supply, divisibility, redemption, cancellation, evidence, governance, and lifecycle finality; conversely, two semantically equivalent tokens may be implemented on different ledgers and through different transaction models. This paper presents the InterWork Alliance Token Taxonomy Framework (TTF) as a typed semantic composition model for implementation neutral token specifications. We formalize TTF artifacts, token formulas, behavior and property-set composition, well-formedness constraints, and a layered conformance model covering formula, artifact, message, state, and trace conformance. We further define a reference validation procedure and show how platform neutral control messages can induce conformance obligations and implementation tests. The model is evaluated analytically through document-token, warehouse-receipt, and carbon/digital-MRV case studies, together with representative invalid compositions that interface standards alone do not expose. The analysis shows that token semantics can be specified, compared, validated, mapped, and governed independently of platform binding, providing a foundation for more reliable token interoperability across smart contract platforms, permissioned ledgers, and shared-state systems.

</details>


### [5] [Design and Operation of a Federated GPU Cluster for Digital Humanities within DHinfra.at](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10552)
*Florian Atzenhofer-Baumgartner,David Fleischhacker,Max Resch,Lukas Waldhofer,Michael Otto*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文介绍为数字人文研究构建的小型联邦GPU集群的设计、实现与运行经验，并给出低预算领域专用集群的建设指南。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字人文研究需要GPU算力支持大语言模型等应用，但小型团队常受限于预算、功耗与人力，且缺乏可参考的领域专用集群建设经验。

Method: 构建跨两所大学站点的联邦GPU集群：接入国家身份联邦实现登录代理，提供交互式笔记本、SSH和兼容OpenAI的推理API三类接口；核心是控制平面，将联邦身份、按项目配额和模型目录映射到自助服务界面，并采用请求-审批队列管理所有特权操作；模型服务采用两级架构，结合常驻服务与按需模型切换，使研究者无需集群账号即可通过HTTPS使用大语言模型。

Result: 报告了塑造该系统的约束条件，包括固定功耗预算、欧盟范围采购、约1.5个全职等效人员的团队规模，以及优先访问与利用率之间的关系；同时记录了主要建设决策及未采用的替代方案；平台以Apache License 2.0发布。

Conclusion: 为在有限预算下构建领域专用GPU集群的团队提供了可操作的建设手册，并以持续更新的技术报告形式随平台演进迭代。

Abstract: We describe the design, implementation, and operation of a small federated GPU cluster built for Digital Humanities (DH) research within the Austrian DHinfra.at project. The system spans two university sites, brokers logins from a national identity federation, and exposes compute through three interfaces: interactive notebooks, SSH, and an OpenAI-compatible inference API. The core of the platform is a control plane that maps federated identity, per-project quotas, and a model catalogue onto self-service interfaces. We document this control plane in detail, including the request-and-approval queue that mediates every privileged cluster effect and the two-tier model-serving setup, which combines always-on services with on-demand model swapping and lets researchers consume large language models over HTTPS without holding a cluster account. We report the constraints that shaped the build: a fixed power budget, EU-wide procurement, a team of about 1.5 full-time equivalents, and the relationship between prioritized access and utilization. We also record the main build decisions against the alternatives we researched and did not adopt. The platform is released under the Apache License 2.0. We close with a playbook for teams building a domain-specific GPU cluster on a limited budget. This is a living technical report, versioned and updated as the platform evolves.

</details>


### [6] [Memory Profiling and Migration for Heterogeneous Memory Architectures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10554)
*Marios Asiminakis,Polydoros Petrakis,Manolis Marazakis*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: SHAMBLES 是一个内核集成、无需改动应用的异构内存框架，通过低开销剖析与可插拔策略自动在 HBM 与 DRAM 间迁移数据，在 HPCG、DGEMM、Himeno 上以部分 HBM 占用维持接近全 HBM 的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异构内存（HBM+商用DRAM）可加速带宽受限的HPC负载，但现有页放置依赖手工调优或面向多级动态设计的OS启发式，缺乏透明度与可移植性，需要开发者介入。

Method: 提出SHAMBLES：内核集成框架，低开销剖析应用访存行为，跨层迁移数据且无需修改应用。提供策略无关接口与轻量用户态运行时，支持可插拔策略（如基于最近使用与频率）及用于对照研究的静态放置；日志模式记录分配与迁移时间线以便复现分析。在HBM与DDR作为NUMA节点的商用Linux上实现，用HPCG、DGEMM与Himeno mini-app评测。

Result: HPCG中仅保留40%问题规模于HBM，可达全HBM基线的93.75%性能；DGEMM中动态策略保持仅三分之一矩阵占用于HBM，达到全HBM性能的99%；Himeno表明快速层选择需兼顾工作负载与规模，50%快速层预算下L规模优于全HBM与全DDR放置，而XL规模则回归倾向HBM。

Conclusion: 透明、策略驱动的迁移能响应访存局部性变化，将热数据集中于HBM而无需开发者干预，为分层内存上的性能可移植性提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Heterogeneous memory systems that combine high-bandwidth memory (HBM) with commodity DRAM can accelerate bandwidth-bound HPC workloads, but current page placement largely depends on manual tuning or OS heuristics not designed for multi-tier dynamics. We present SHAMBLES, a kernel-integrated framework that profiles application memory behavior at low overhead and migrates data across tiers without requiring application changes. SHAMBLES exposes a policy-agnostic interface and a lightweight user-space runtime with pluggable policies (e.g. recency and frequency based) as well as static placement for controlled studies. A logging mode provides reproducible timelines of allocations and migrations to aid analysis. We implement SHAMBLES on a commodity Linux system with HBM and DDR exposed as NUMA nodes and evaluate it with the HPCG, DGEMM benchmarks and Himeno stencil mini-app. Our design and methodology show how transparent, policy-driven migration can respond to changing access locality and concentrate hot data in HBM without developer intervention, offering a practical path to performance portability on tiered memory. Results from HPCG show that we can maintain up to 93.75% of the all-in-HBM baseline performance, while keeping only 40% of the problem size in the HBM. DGEMM experiments show that dynamic policies in SHAMBLES sustain up to 99% of the all-in-HBM performance, while keeping only one third of the DGEMM matrix footprint in HBM. For Himeno, SHAMBLES shows that fast-tier selection must be both workload-aware and size-aware: with a 50% fast-tier budget, it can outperform fixed all-in-HBM and all-in-DDR placements for the L size, while the XL size shifts back toward HBM.

</details>


### [7] [Composable CXL Memory as a Kubernetes-Native Shared Memory for LLM Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10790)
*Hongjian Fan,Kevin Zhang,David Habinsky,Sean Dykstra*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个Kubernetes DRA驱动，将可组合CXL内存变为可调度的集群资源，用于LLM服务中跨节点KV-cache复用，跨节点前缀复用使TTFT降低5.5–36.6倍，共享开销仅1–4%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM服务中KV-cache复用通常局限于节点本地（GPU前缀缓存、CPU-DRAM卸载），跨节点的共享内存复用能力缺失，导致不同节点上的副本无法共享前缀计算结果，必须回退到完整重算。作者希望利用可组合CXL内存实现内存解耦，把跨节点共享内存作为可调度的集群资源来支持KV-cache复用。

Method: 设计一个Kubernetes DRA驱动：按需组合CXL区域，在各参与主机上物化为DAX设备，并以单一CDI名称注入Pod，使不同节点上的Pod访问同一物理区域。为vLLM/llm-d提供共享内存连接器，将该区域用作KV-cache层，并在共享介质内嵌入槽目录（slot directory），从而无需外部元数据服务。在双节点集群（512 GiB CXL设备、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct）上进行验证。

Result: 在外部命中率95.4–99.5%时，跨节点前缀复用将TTFT降低5.5倍至36.6倍；而节点本地层（GPU前缀缓存、CPU-DRAM卸载）则回退到完整重算。跨节点与同节点复用的延迟比（共享差距）为1–4%，说明跨节点复用相对同节点复用几乎不引入额外延迟。两个副本均运行完整引擎。

Conclusion: 该研究展示了内存解耦（memory disaggregation）而非预填充/解码解耦的可行性，将可组合CXL内存作为Kubernetes可调度资源支持跨节点KV-cache复用，显著降低TTFT且共享开销极小。作者将其定位为可行性研究而非性能评估。

Abstract: We present a Kubernetes Dynamic Resource Allocation (DRA) driver that makes composable CXL memory a schedulable cluster resource, and evaluate the resulting shared-memory tier for cross-node KV-cache reuse in LLM serving. The driver composes CXL regions on demand, materializes them as DAX devices on each participating host, and injects them into pods under a single Container Device Interface (CDI) name so that pods on different nodes access the same physical region. A shared-memory connector for vLLM/llm-d uses that region as a KV-cache tier with a slot directory embedded inside the shared medium, which eliminates the need for an external metadata service. On a two-node cluster with a 512\,GiB CXL appliance and Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, cross-node prefix reuse reduces TTFT by 5.5$\times$--36.6$\times$ at an external hit rate of 95.4--99.5\,\%, while node-local tiers (GPU prefix caching, CPU-DRAM offload) fall back to full recompute. The sharing gap, defined as the latency ratio between cross-node and same-node reuse, is 1--4\%, indicating that cross-node reuse incurs little additional latency relative to same-node reuse on our testbed. Both replicas run full engines; the study demonstrates memory disaggregation rather than prefill/decode disaggregation. We report this as a feasibility study rather than a performance evaluation.

</details>


### [8] [ExaServe: Large-Scale LLM Serving on Exascale HPC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10812)
*Wenyi Wang,Shu Shi,Yadu Babuji,Ian Foster,Kyle Chard*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 ExaServe 框架，可将声明式 YAML 转化为可复现的大规模 LLM 服务部署，并在 Aurora 超算上从 1 扩展到 256 节点进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云原生 LLM 服务框架在数据中心已常规化，但在领阶级超算上部署仍面临调度器集成、MPI 启动、加速器选择、节点本地权重暂存及平台特定补丁等工程挑战。

Method: ExaServe 是一个可 pip 安装的框架，将声明式 YAML 规范转换为可复现的大规模 LLM 服务部署，处理调度、启动、加速器选择与权重暂存等环节。

Result: 在 ALCF Aurora 上部署 1 至 256 节点（3072 个 vLLM 副本）。非流式推理近线性扩展至 256 节点，达 27.1k 请求/秒（3.8M tokens/秒）；令牌流式受限于集中式代理，约 4.7k 请求/秒；并发现 Ray Serve 控制平面 O(N²) 瓶颈，256 节点时集群启动约需 30 分钟。

Conclusion: ExaServe 提供了实用、可复现的部署路径，同时揭示了未来百亿亿级 LLM 服务的关键障碍。

Abstract: Cloud-native LLM serving frameworks have made deployment routine in data centers, yet deploying them on leadership-class supercomputers remains an engineering challenge requiring scheduler integration, MPI launch, accelerator selection, node-local weight staging, and platform-specific patches. We present \textit{ExaServe}, a pip-installable framework that transforms a declarative YAML specification into a reproducible large-scale LLM serving deployment. Using ExaServe, we deploy LLM serving on ALCF Aurora from 1 to 256 nodes (3072 vLLM replicas). Non-streaming inference scales nearly linearly to 256 nodes, reaching 27.1\,k requests/s (3.8\,M tokens/s). Token streaming scales differently: a centralized proxy plateaus at $\sim$4.7\,k requests/s despite the model servers remaining within the service-level objective. We also identify an \emph{O}(\emph{N}\textsuperscript{2}) Ray Serve control-plane bottleneck that increases cluster bring-up to $\sim$30 minutes at 256 nodes. ExaServe provides a practical, reproducible deployment path while exposing key barriers to future exascale LLM serving.

</details>


### [9] [Navigating Small-World Networks with Distance Predictions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10885)
*Ladan Kian,Ming Ming Tan,Dariusz Kowalski*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究带预测信息的贪婪路由：在增广格网上，转发代理依据每步从预言机重新抽取的含噪预测（有坐标感知时预测图距离，无坐标感知时预测格距离）选择邻居。前者实现 O(log n/(1-4kεδ)) 期望投递时间，渐进优于经典 Θ(log² n)；后者在节点完全不披露坐标时仍以 O(n/(1-4kεδ)) 期望步数到达目标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Kleinberg 用小世界模型给出了去中心化贪婪路由的算法基础，但经典设定中节点只能获取有限信息，投递时间为 Θ(log² n)。作者希望探究：如果额外提供"正确类型"的少量预测信息，能否突破经典界；以及在隐私保护场景中节点完全不披露坐标时，可靠投递是否仍然可行。

Method: 在增广 k 维格网上研究 predicted-greedy routing：移动代理每步走向使到目标距离的含噪 (ε,δ)-预测最小化的邻居，该预测在每步由一个以完整路由历史为条件的预言机重新抽取。分两种情形：一是有坐标感知的代理，可精确计算格距离但无法计算含捷径网络中的图距离，改为使用图距离的 (ε,δ)-预测；二是无任何坐标感知的代理，连格距离也无法计算，改用格距离的 (ε,δ)-预测。

Result: 有坐标感知并获图距离预测时，期望投递时间为 O(log n/(1-4kεδ))，渐进优于经典 Θ(log² n)；完全无坐标感知、仅获格距离预测时，期望步数为 O(n/(1-4kεδ))，仍能可靠到达目标。

Conclusion: 适量且类型正确的预测信息足以使去中心化路由显著快于 Kleinberg 的经典界；即便节点完全不披露坐标，也能实现可靠投递。

Abstract: The small-world phenomenon was given an algorithmic foundation by Kleinberg, who showed that in an augmented $k$-dimensional lattice a decentralized greedy algorithm delivers a message in $O(\log^2 n)$ expected steps. We study predicted-greedy routing, in which a mobile agent forwarding the message moves at each step to the neighbor minimizing a noisy $(\varepsilon,δ)$-prediction of its distance to the target, redrawn at every step from an oracle conditioned on the full routing history. Two cases arise from what this agent can observe. An agent with the coordinate awareness can still compute lattice distance exactly, but not graph distance in the shortcut-augmented network, since that depends on the shortcuts of nodes it has not yet visited; given an $(\varepsilon,δ)$-prediction of graph distance, information the classical model never supplies, it achieves expected delivery time $O(\log n/(1-4k\varepsilonδ))$, an asymptotic improvement over $Θ(\log^2 n)$. An agent with no coordinate awareness at all, the natural model for a privacy-preserving network whose nodes never disclose their coordinates, cannot compute even lattice distance; given an $(\varepsilon,δ)$-prediction of lattice distance instead, it still reaches the target in $O(n/(1-4k\varepsilonδ))$ expected steps. Together these results show that a modest amount of predicted information, of the right kind, is enough to accelerate decentralized routing well below Kleinberg's classical bound, and that even when nodes reveal no coordinates at all, reliable delivery remains achievable.

</details>


### [10] [Online Treasure Hunt in Vertex-Permuted Dynamic Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11013)
*Kamran Ayoubi,Bernard Mans,Lata Narayanan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文研究了顶点置换动态环（VP）中多智能体寻宝问题，给出了可行性与搜索时间的紧界，并比较了随机模型下不同对手的期望复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在顶点随时间被置换的动态环中，探索与寻宝的可行性及效率尚不清晰，尤其缺乏对智能体数量、置换约束（如VP(δ)）以及随机置换对手模型下的系统刻画。本文旨在建立这些设定下的不可能性、可行条件与紧界。

Method: 先在一般VP模型下证明k≤n−3个智能体无法完成寻宝；再引入VP(δ)约束，证明仅当δ≥⌈(n−1)/2⌉时可行；对单智能体给出最坏搜索时间与竞争比的Θ(δn)紧界（δ≥2n），并设计k智能体的最优算法；最后在R-VP随机置换模型下分析遗忘型与自适应对手的期望步数。

Result: 一般VP中k≤n−3不可行；VP(δ)仅δ≥⌈(n−1)/2⌉可行；单智能体最坏搜索时间与竞争比为Θ(δn)（δ≥2n）；k智能体最优算法实现k倍加速；R-VP下期望步数为Θ(n)（遗忘型对手）与Θ(n log n)（自适应对手）。

Conclusion: 论文完整刻画了顶点置换动态环中寻宝的可行性边界与搜索复杂度，表明智能体数量、边重现间隔δ以及随机置换对手类型共同决定问题难度，并给出了匹配上下界的算法。

Abstract: We study the problem of treasure hunt by a group of $k \geq 1$ agents in vertex-permuted dynamic rings (VP). In this model, the $n$ vertices remain on a ring but are permuted at each time step. We first show that treasure hunt is impossible for any $k \leq n-3$ agents, if there are no restrictions on the sequence of permutations used in the dynamic ring. We then study the $VP(δ)$ setting, in which for every pair $i, j$ of vertices, the edge $(i, j)$ is guaranteed to appear within $δ$ steps. We show that the class $VP(δ)$ is feasible only for $δ\geq \left\lceil \frac{n-1}{2}\right\rceil$. For the one-agent case, we show a tight bound of $Θ(δn)$ on the worst-case search time as well as competitive ratio of any online algorithm for treasure hunt, provided $δ\geq 2n$. We then give an optimal algorithm for $k$ agents, thereby showing that $k$ agents can obtain a speedup of $k$ on the worst-case search time. Finally, in the R-VP setting, in which in every step, the vertices are arranged as a ring according to a random permutation, we show that treasure hunt takes expected $Θ(n)$ steps against an oblivious adversary and $Θ(n \log n)$ steps against an adaptive adversary.

</details>


### [11] [The Computing Channel: How Modulation Programs the Airwaves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11145)
*Saeed Razavikia,Carlo Fischione*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出"数字函数导向通信"框架，通过联合设计有限字母表符号表示与接收端判决，使多址叠加直接编码目标函数，从而避免先恢复各设备消息，实现数字化的空中计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分布式计算与分布式人工智能需要频繁交换中间结果，但许多应用只需聚合结果而非各设备的具体消息。传统系统先恢复每条消息再计算聚合，而空中计算(OAC)利用同时传输直接获得聚合。然而主流OAC依赖模拟信令，与有限精度数据和数字通信流程存在不匹配，因此需要一种数字化的函数导向通信方案。

Method: 提出数字函数导向通信：将有限字母表符号表示与接收机判决联合设计，使多址叠加直接编码所需函数，无需恢复单个输入。文中引入计算星座原理，并讨论主要设计方法、扩展及实现挑战；以联邦边缘学习为例说明该框架如何在量化模型更新上直接运行。

Result: 以联邦边缘学习为例，展示了该框架能够减少依赖用户的数据承载资源，同时可直接在量化模型更新上运行，兼容数字通信流程与有限精度数据。

Conclusion: 数字函数导向通信通过计算星座与收发联合设计，为分布式计算与分布式AI提供了一种数字化的函数聚合方案，克服了模拟OAC与数字系统的不匹配问题，并具有在联邦边缘学习等场景的应用潜力。

Abstract: Distributed computing and distributed artificial intelligence require frequent exchanges of intermediate results, although many applications need only an aggregate rather than messages from individual devices. Conventional systems recover each message before computing the aggregate, whereas over-the-air computation (OAC) exploits simultaneous transmission to obtain it directly. However, dominant OAC implementations rely on analog signaling, creating a mismatch with finite-precision data and digital communication procedures. This article presents digital function-oriented communication, in which finite-alphabet symbol representations and receiver decisions are jointly designed so that multiple-access superposition encodes the desired function without recovering individual inputs. We introduce its computational-constellation principle, main design approaches, extensions, and implementation challenges. Federated edge learning illustrates how the framework can reduce user-dependent data-bearing resources while operating directly on quantized model updates.

</details>


### [12] [Can AI Remediate Backend Failures Safely? GuardedAct with Blast-Radius-Aware Sandboxing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11264)
*Wanrong Cai,Tianyu Yu,Shaorui Pi,Xiaoxuan Sun,Wenrui Ma*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 提出 GuardedAct——一个"沙箱优先"的修复框架，在 LLM 动作生成器与生产环境之间插入基于爆炸半径的验证层，通过数字孪生沙箱模拟与回滚置信门控，实现安全可控的自动修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM 能针对微服务故障生成修复动作，但直接在生产环境执行 AI 生成的修复动作会带来级联的连带损害风险。因此需要一种机制，在动作真正落地生产前进行风险验证。

Method: GuardedAct 分为四个阶段：(1) 摄入诊断报告、实时系统拓扑与近期遥测数据；(2) 提示 LLM 生成按优先级排序的候选修复动作列表；(3) 在轻量级数字孪生沙箱中模拟每个动作，估计其爆炸半径并赋予风险标签；(4) 执行回滚置信门控，仅自动执行低风险动作，高风险动作上报人工复核。

Result: 在 DeathStarBench 社交网络应用中注入的五个故障场景上评估：GuardAct 总体恢复率达 87.4%，相比直接 LLM 执行将连带损害从 25.6% 降至 5.2%（降低 79.7%），代价是沙箱引入的平均恢复时间增加约 8 秒。消融实验证实各组件对安全—速度权衡均有实质性贡献。

Conclusion: GuardedAct 通过沙箱验证与风险门控，在可接受的恢复时间开销下显著提升 LLM 自动修复的安全性，验证了"先模拟、后执行"范式在微服务故障修复中的有效性。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promising capabilities in generating remediation actions for microservice failures. However, directly executing AI-generated repair actions in production risks cascading collateral damage. We propose GuardedAct, a sandbox-first remediation framework that interposes a blast-radius-aware verification layer between the LLM action generator and the production environment. GuardedAct operates in four phases: (1) ingesting a diagnosis report together with the live system topology and recent telemetry, (2) prompting an LLM to produce a ranked list of candidate remediation actions, (3) simulating each action in a lightweight digital-twin sandbox that estimates the blast radius and assigns a risk label, and (4) enforcing a rollback-confidence gate that auto-executes only low-risk actions while escalating high-risk ones for human review. We evaluate GuardedAct on five fault scenarios injected into the DeathStarBench social-network application. Experimental results show that GuardedAct achieves an overall recovery rate of 87.4% while reducing collateral damage by 79.7% relative to direct LLM execution (from 25.6% to 5.2%), at the cost of a modest sandbox-induced increase in mean time to recovery (approximately 8 s). Ablation studies confirm that each component contributes meaningfully to the safety-speed trade-off.

</details>


### [13] [Rethinking Sparse Formats for RISC-V: A Hierarchical Approach to High-Performance SpMV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11352)
*Anna Pirova,Anastasia Vodeneeva,Konstantin Kovalev,Alexander Ustinov,Maksim Zagriadskov,Daniil Litvyakov,Arthur Kulik,Evgeny Kozinov,Valentin Volokitin,Iosif Meyerov*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 比较8种稀疏矩阵存储格式的向量化SpMV实现，提出HCSR格式，在RISC-V上平均加速1.6x且性能最优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SpMV是线性代数的核心计算内核，是科学工程仿真中迭代求解器的基础构件。不同稀疏矩阵存储格式对SpMV性能影响显著，而RISC-V平台上尚缺乏系统的格式比较与优化方案，因此需要对比现有格式并针对RISC-V架构提出改进。

Method: 基于RVV 1.0 intrinsics实现八种主流稀疏矩阵存储格式的向量化SpMV，并提出CSR格式的新型改进——分层CSR（HCSR）。实现以开源C++库RVVLASparse形式公开，并在SpacemiT K1与K3 RISC-V开发板上进行实验。

Result: 实验表明，选择合适的矩阵存储格式可使SpMV平均加速1.6x；在广泛的稀疏矩阵类别中，所提出的HCSR格式在全部对比格式中取得最短执行时间。

Conclusion: 存储格式选择对RISC-V平台SpMV性能有显著影响，HCSR格式在多数稀疏矩阵上表现最佳，验证了针对架构定制格式优化的有效性，相关实现已开源可供复用。

Abstract: The sparse matrix-vector multiplication (SpMV) algorithm is a fundamental computational kernel of linear algebra and serves as a building block for numerous applications, primarily iterative solvers for systems of linear equations used in scientific and engineering simulations. This paper compares vectorized implementations of the SpMV algorithm across eight established sparse matrix storage formats and proposes a novel modification of the CSR format, Hierarchical CSR (HCSR), which enhances SpMV performance on RISC-V processors. Our SpMV implementations utilize RVV 1.0 intrinsics and are publicly available as an open-source C++ library named RVVLASparse. Computational experiments conducted on SpacemiT K1 and K3 RISC-V boards demonstrate that selecting an appropriate matrix storage format accelerates SpMV computations by an average of 1.6x, while the proposed HCSR format achieves the shortest execution time among all considered formats across a broad class of sparse matrices.

</details>


### [14] [Taming Bitwise Behavior in GPU Kernels with Tensor Core: Black-Box Reconstruction, Compiler Enforcement, and Static Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11356)
*Ziteng Yang,Nicholas J. Riasanovsky,Warren Deng,Vivek Sarkar*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文研究GPU核函数浮点归约顺序导致的逐位不一致问题，提出GEMM归约顺序描述符、平衡树归约强制与数据布局优化，以及跨PTX/GCN的逐位等价静态检查器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习系统对GPU核函数的确定性与数值可复现性需求日增，但即便是“确定性”实现，同一核函数仍可能逐位不同。主因是浮点归约顺序，此外还有部分和精度、FMA与舍入位置等。这些选择可能来自手工编码、Triton等块级语言或cuBLAS/rocBLAS等闭源库，且为性能选择的tile形状也决定了算术行为，可能破坏批次不变性。固定顺序代价可达20%，而自动调优器无法识别哪些配置是逐位等价的。

Method: 1) 刻画决定归约与GEMM逐位行为的因素，提出GEMM归约顺序描述符（含split-K的K划分），并据此首次对闭源库算术进行黑盒重建；2) 在Triton lowering中强制平衡树归约，并引入数据布局优化；3) 开发可靠的静态检查器，用于判定编译后GPU核函数间的逐位等价，并首次覆盖NVIDIA PTX与AMD GCN，集成进Triton自动调优器以将搜索限制在单一逐位等价类。

Result: 其Triton GEMM族在Blackwell与Hopper的所有测试用例中与NVIDIA cuBLAS逐位匹配；在带融合epilogue的真实LLM形状上，性能达到或超过torch.compile。数据布局优化使GB300与H100上27个核函数中的19个进入自由顺序性能的10%以内。所开发的检查器可跨PTX与GCN判定逐位等价。

Conclusion: 浮点归约顺序是GPU核函数逐位差异的核心来源，通过归约顺序描述符、平衡树归约与数据布局优化以及静态逐位等价检查，可在较小性能代价下实现确定且可复现的GPU计算，并有效约束自动调优的搜索空间。

Abstract: Determinism and numerical reproducibility are increasingly required of GPU kernels in machine learning systems, yet deterministic implementations of the same kernel can still differ bit for bit. Floating-point reduction order is the primary cause, alongside partial-sum precision, fused multiply-add operations, and rounding placement. These choices may be hand-coded, selected by a block-level language such as Triton, or hidden inside a closed-source library such as cuBLAS or rocBLAS. A tile shape chosen for performance therefore also determines the arithmetic, potentially breaking batch invariance. Preserving a fixed order can cost up to 20 percent, while an autotuner cannot identify which configurations are bitwise equivalent.
  We characterize the factors determining the bitwise behavior of reductions and general matrix multiplication (GEMM). First, we introduce a descriptor of GEMM reduction order, including the partitioning of K in split-K GEMM. Using it, we perform the first black-box reconstruction of a closed-source library's arithmetic for bit-level correctness. Our family of Triton GEMMs matches NVIDIA cuBLAS in all tested cases on Blackwell and Hopper. For realistic LLM shapes with fused epilogues, it matches or exceeds torch.compile performance. Second, we enforce balanced-tree reduction during Triton lowering and introduce a data-layout optimization that brings 19 of 27 kernels on GB300 and H100 within 10 percent of free-order performance. Third, we develop sound static checkers for bitwise equivalence between compiled GPU kernels, including the first checker spanning NVIDIA PTX and AMD GCN. Integrated into Triton's autotuner, the checker restricts search to a single bit-equivalence class.

</details>


### [15] [Entwine: Coordinating Tiled Computation and Fine-Grained Communication across GPUs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11562)
*Kai Ma,Quanfeng Lv,Jingguo Ge,Bowei Dai,Kefan Ruan*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 本文提出 Entwine，通过协调 tile 计算顺序、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配，缓解计算与通信进度不匹配问题，在张量并行 LLM 负载上相较 cuBLAS+NCCL 取得 1.232x 的几何平均加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代 GPU 计算将张量分块以利用数据重用与并行性，单个 tile 计算会早于整个张量完成，从而产生计算与通信重叠的机会。然而计算与通信进度不匹配会限制这种机会：通信在数据未就绪时停滞，在数据突增时滞后，同时通信还会占用共享资源从而拖慢计算，抵消重叠收益。

Method: Entwine 协调 tile 计算顺序、细粒度通信和 SM 资源分配以最小化整体完成时间。它重排 tile 计算顺序，使数据以更规律的节奏产生以供通信；将这种调度与基于 SM 的细粒度通信耦合，以低延迟、低开销处理 tile 结果；由于通信 kernel 也消耗 SM 资源，Entwine 协调二者的资源分配，在通信进度与计算减速之间取得平衡。

Result: 在具有代表性的张量并行 LLM 负载上，Entwine 相较 cuBLAS+NCCL 取得几何平均 1.232x（最高 1.433x）的加速，并在几何平均上优于最先进的重叠基线 3.1%–9.8%。

Conclusion: 通过联合优化 tile 计算顺序、细粒度通信与 SM 资源分配，Entwine 有效缓解了计算与通信进度不匹配的问题，显著提升了张量并行 LLM 负载的整体性能。实现将在论文发表后开源。

Abstract: Modern high-performance GPU computations partition tensors into tiles to exploit data reuse and parallelism. Individual tile computations complete earlier than the full tensor computation, creating opportunities to overlap computation and communication. However, a mismatch between computation and communication progress can limit these opportunities. Communication stalls when no data is ready, and may lag when data arrives in bursts. Communication can also slow computation by consuming shared resources, offsetting the benefits of overlap.
  We present Entwine, which coordinates tile computation order, fine-grained communication, and SM resource allocation to minimize overall completion time. Entwine reorders tile computation to produce data for communication at a more regular pace. Entwine couples this schedule with fine-grained SM-based communication to process tile results with low latency and low overhead. Since the communication kernel also consumes SM resources, Entwine coordinates their allocation to balance communication progress against computation slowdown. Across representative tensor-parallel LLM workloads, Entwine achieves a geomean speedup of 1.232x (up to 1.433x) over cuBLAS+NCCL, and outperforms state-of-the-art overlap baselines by 3.1-9.8% in geomean. We will open-source our implementation upon publication.

</details>


### [16] [Building py-kvcache: A Performance Characterization of External KV Caching for vLLM with NVMe SSDs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11744)
*Joseph Kanichai,Tiziano De Matteis,Animesh Trivedi*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: 该论文刻画了vLLM中前缀缓存（GPU/CPU/NVMe分层）的性能权衡，并提出py-kvcache系统，通过异步直接I/O、有界共享暂存和调度器感知预取来提升长上下文请求的TTFT，在磁盘加载上比LMCache快2.0倍，并指出外部KV缓存应作为场景相关的准入决策。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前缀缓存可复用已计算的KV状态以降低长上下文请求的首token时间（TTFT），但对短前缀或快速GPU而言，重新计算可能比从外部缓存加载更快。现有认知仅关注设备带宽，缺乏对不同存储层级和调度时机的系统刻画，因此需要理解缓存何时真正有益。

Method: 作者在vLLM上跨GPU、CPU和NVMe层级，使用合成负载、长上下文基准、生产trace进行性能刻画，分析传输粒度、中间内存使用以及传输进入请求调度的时机的影响。基于此提出py-kvcache——一个vLLM KV Offload连接器，具备异步直接I/O、有界共享暂存和调度器感知预加载，可在请求仍等待时启动磁盘读取并与计算重叠。

Result: 在80k token时，py-kvcache从磁盘加载比LMCache快2.0倍，其中预加载贡献1.34倍。同时启用GPU、CPU和磁盘缓存时比LMCache快1.23倍，与原生vLLM KV Offload实现差距约4%。LongBench和SCBench显示该收益可扩展到不规则前缀链和多轮负载。Bailian trace回放中，在较弱GPU上改善了TTFT，但在H100上平均请求低于盈亏平衡点，仅GPU内存已能保留足够前缀。

Conclusion: 外部KV缓存的收益取决于传输粒度、内存使用和调度时机，而非仅设备带宽。因此外部KV缓存应被视为依赖具体配置的准入决策。py-kvcache在适合场景下显著优于LMCache，并接近原生实现，代码已开源。

Abstract: Prefix caching can reduce the time to first token (TTFT) of long-context LLM requests by reusing previously computed key-value (KV) states, but for short prefixes or fast GPUs, recomputation can be faster than loading from an external cache. We characterize this tradeoff in vLLM across GPU, CPU, and NVMe tiers using synthetic workloads, long-context benchmarks, production traces, and find that cache performance depends on transfer granularity, intermediate memory use, and when transfers enter the request schedule, not only on device bandwidth. These findings motivate py-kvcache, a vLLM KV Offload connector with asynchronous direct I/O, bounded shared staging, and scheduler-aware preloading, which starts disk reads while requests are still waiting, overlapping with compute. At 80k tokens, py-kvcache loading from disk is 2.0x faster than LMCache, with preloading contributing 1.34x. With GPU, CPU, and disk caching enabled, it is 1.23x faster than LMCache and within approximately 4% of the native vLLM KV Offload implementation. LongBench and SCBench show that these benefits extend to irregular prefix chains and multi-turn workloads. Bailian trace replays improve TTFT on a weaker GPU, but on an H100 the average request falls below the break-even point and GPU memory alone retains enough prefixes. External KV caching should therefore be treated as a setup specific admission decision. The py-kvcacheimplementation is available at: https://github.com/atlarge-research/py-kvcache.

</details>


### [17] [GPU-CFR: 80x Faster Counterfactual Regret Minimization by Compiling the Game to Static Dataflow and CUDA Graph Replay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11923)
*Boning Li,Longbo Huang*

Main category: cs.DC

TL;DR: GPU-CFR通过将博弈的CFR迭代编译为静态数据流并用CUDA Graph Replay执行，大幅消除GPU上的内核启动与框架开销，在多种棋牌类游戏上显著超越现有GPU和CPU实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CFR是少数在GPU上仍慢于CPU的大规模数值工作负载。每次迭代需遍历含数十亿状态、由大量细小且相互依赖的gather/scatter步骤组成的博弈树，并通过通用树接口下发；在GPU上每个内核仅运行数微秒，导致内核启动和框架调度主导运行时间，此前GPU实现均不敌优化后的CPU代码。

Method: 作者观察到对于固定博弈，除数值外CFR迭代的一切在首次运行前均已确定。据此提出GPU-CFR编译器与运行时：将博弈一次性编译为静态数据流，用扁平边与信息集数组、预计算索引和按深度分批的通行固定整个操作序列，迭代间仅求解器状态变化。采用静态机会折叠、深度级执行块和双通道reach缓冲区，将框架操作数最多减少18.1倍。因形状、索引与缓冲地址不变，用CUDA Graph Replay记录一次迭代并以单次图启动重放。

Result: 在单张A100上，涵盖卡牌、骰子与棋盘游戏的八游戏套件中，GPU-CFR比同加速器上最快的既有GPU CFR快29.8–80.4倍，在四个最大游戏上比最快的开源CPU实现之一LiteEFG快14–258倍。编译表示贡献了大部分优势：在无加速器的八个CPU线程上已比GPU基线快2.2–51.1倍。CPU优化路径按位复现参考迭代，树构建与图捕获在首次求解内即可回本。

Conclusion: 在不改变更新规则的前提下，GPU-CFR在套件的中大型游戏上击败所有CPU与GPU基线，证明静态数据流编译与图重放是加速CFR的有效途径。

Abstract: Counterfactual regret minimization (CFR) is one of the few large numerical workloads that still runs faster on CPUs than on GPUs. Each iteration sweeps a game tree with up to billions of states in millions of small, interdependent gather and scatter steps issued through a generic tree interface. On a GPU every kernel finishes in microseconds, so kernel launches and framework dispatch dominate the run time, and prior GPU implementations have lost to optimized CPU code. We observe that for a fixed game, everything about a CFR iteration except the numerical values is known before the first iteration runs. We propose GPU-CFR, a compiler and runtime built on this observation. It compiles any game once into static dataflow: flat edge and information-set arrays, precomputed indices, and depth-level batched passes fix the entire operation sequence, and only solver state changes between iterations. Static chance folding, depth-level execution blocks, and a dual-lane reach buffer cut the number of framework operations by up to 18.1x. Because shapes, indices, and buffer addresses never change, CUDA Graph Replay records the iteration once and replays it with a single graph launch. On one A100, across an eight-game suite that spans card games, dice games, and board games, GPU-CFR runs 29.8--80.4x faster than the fastest prior GPU CFR on the same accelerator, and 14--258x faster than LiteEFG, one of the fastest open-source CPU implementations, on the four largest games. The compiled representation carries most of that margin: on eight CPU threads with no accelerator it is already 2.2--51.1x faster than the GPU baseline. On the CPU the optimized path reproduces the reference iterates bitwise, and tree construction and graph capture pay for themselves within the first solve. GPU-CFR beats every CPU and GPU baseline on the mid-to-large games of the suite without changing the update rule.

</details>

### [18] [When Synthetic Data Hurts: On Catastrophic Forgetting in Skill Retrieval for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10750)
*Syed Shariyar Murtaza,Yifan Nie,Utkarsh Soni,Eugene Wen,Arvid Frydenlund*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个覆盖34,396个技能的生产级技能路由器，并系统研究了在真实监督稀缺、依赖合成数据场景下的技能检索问题。实验发现合成数据微调能提升同分布检索，但会造成对真实及OOD数据的灾难性遗忘；引入持续学习方法（嵌入锚正则、LwF、EWC、L2初始化）可缓解遗忘，并在0.6B Qwen检索器与重排器上将合成同分布技能检索提升13.98%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体日益依赖运行时检索的外部技能，从大规模技能库中准确选择技能成为关键挑战；同时真实监督数据稀缺，现有合成数据微调方法可能带来灾难性遗忘问题，缺乏实用的基准与稳健的微调方案。

Method: 在34,396个技能上构建生产级技能路由器，开展大规模技能检索研究；利用有限真实监督与合成数据；评估多种受持续学习启发的遗忘缓解微调方法，包括嵌入锚正则化、Learning without Forgetting (LwF)、Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC) 与L2初始化。

Result: 合成数据微调可提升同分布检索，但导致真实与OOD数据上的灾难性遗忘；所评估的遗忘缓解方法在保留OOD技能检索性能的同时，使0.6B Qwen检索器和重排器的合成同分布技能检索提升13.98%。

Conclusion: 本文为稀缺、多正例监督场景提供了实用的基准与稳健的微调配方，验证了持续学习式正则化在缓解灾难性遗忘、兼顾OOD与同分布检索性能方面的有效性。

Abstract: LLM agents increasingly rely on external skills retrieved at runtime, making skill selection from large repositories a critical challenge. We present a production skill router over 34,396 skills and a large-scale study of skill retrieval using limited real supervision and synthetic data. We found that the synthetic-data fine-tuning improves in-distribution retrieval but it causes catastrophic forgetting on real and out-of-distribution (OOD) data. We evaluate several forgetting mitigation fine-tuning approaches inspired by continual learning, including embedding-anchor regularization, Learning without Forgetting (LwF), Elastic Weight Consolidation (EWC), and L2-initialization. The results show that these approaches not only retain the performance on OOD skills retrieval but also improve the retrieval on synthetic in-distribution skills by 13.98\% for 0.6B Qwen retriever and reranker. Our results provide a practical benchmark and a robust fine-tuning recipe for scarce, multi-positive supervision.

</details>


### [19] [UniRec: Cross-stage Multi-Task Fusion with Preference Alignment for Cascaded Recommender Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11052)
*Lingyuan Kong,Jiaqi Cui,Fanjiao Zeng,Congqi Wang,Yu Li,Yuan Cheng,Jingxin Liu,Xiaoshuang Chen,Kaiqiao Zhan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出 UniRec 跨阶段统一推荐融合模型，通过共享嵌入、双轴偏好对齐与属性分组正则，实现预排序与排序阶段联合优化，离线优于基线，线上 app 使用时长提升 0.616%，已在快手全量部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业推荐系统采用多级级联结构，各阶段目标、特征空间与时延约束不同。分别优化预排序与排序会造成跨阶段不一致：上游可能过滤掉下游排序偏好的物品，独立调优的下游融合也会抵消上游改进。现有多任务融合多聚焦排序阶段内部多目标融合，跨阶段方法通常仅在下游为上游加一个分数因子，两阶段融合模块的联合优化尚属空白。

Method: UniRec 包含三点：一是两个融合代理部分共享输入嵌入并在单一计算图中训练，使任一阶段的梯度都能经共享表示影响另一阶段；二是提出双轴偏好对齐目标，纵向跨阶段一致性项将下游成对偏好迁移到上游融合分，横向紧凑聚合项将异构先验信号上的数十个成对目标重组为双向偏好证据；三是针对无约束端到端融合会利用物品属性分布失衡、过度集中于高回报区域的问题，加入属性分组相对正则，在属性组内计算优势并在同组内归一化策略，使整体提升某一高回报组不带来优化收益。

Result: 离线实验中 UniRec 稳定优于单阶段融合与跨阶段协调基线；线上 A/B 测试显示 app 使用时长提升 0.616%；UniRec 已在快手平台全量部署。

Conclusion: UniRec 表明跨阶段融合模块的联合优化可行且有效，共享表示、双轴偏好对齐与属性分组正则可缓解跨阶段不一致与优化失衡，并已在工业场景取得可观线上收益。

Abstract: Industrial recommender systems use cascaded stages with different objectives, feature spaces, and latency constraints. Optimizing pre-ranking and ranking separately can create cross-stage inconsistency: upstream models may filter out items preferred by downstream rankers, and independently tuned downstream fusion can offset upstream improvements. Existing multi-task fusion methods focus on multi-objective fusion within the ranking stage, and cross-stage methods typically only add a downstream score factor to upstream ranking. Joint optimization of fusion modules across both stages remains largely unexplored.
  We propose UniRec, a Unified Cross-stage Recommendation Fusion model. First, the two fusion agents partially share input embeddings and are trained in a single computation graph, so gradients from either stage propagate through the shared representation and influence the other. Second, we introduce a dual-axis preference alignment objective: a vertical cross-stage consistency term transfers downstream pairwise preferences to the upstream fusion score, and a horizontal compact aggregation term reorganizes dozens of pairwise objectives over heterogeneous prior signals into bidirectional preference evidence. Third, we find that unconstrained end-to-end fusion optimization can exploit imbalances in item attribute distributions, over-concentrating on high-reward regions at the cost of other objectives. We therefore add an attribute group-relative regularization that computes advantages within attribute groups and normalizes the policy over the same groups, so uniformly promoting an entire high-reward group yields no optimization gain. Offline, UniRec consistently outperforms single-stage fusion and cross-stage coordination baselines. Online A/B tests show a 0.616\% gain in app usage duration. UniRec is fully deployed on the Kuaishou platform.

</details>


### [20] [VikingRAG: Accurate and Token-efficient Retrieval-augmented Generation over Structured Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11390)
*Peiyuan Gao,Gaoyuan Zhang,Haojie Qin,Yahui Sun,Qianyi Zhang,Yunhao Zhang,Zeyu Wang,Wei Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: VikingRAG 是一个目录感知的语义数据管理系统，通过紧密集成语义与结构访问，实现结构上下文高效、证据缺口驱动的多轮检索，从而在大幅降低 token 成本的同时保持高 RAG 准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有先进的 RAG 方法利用文档结构获取充分证据，但往往带来高昂的 token 成本。如何在不过多牺牲 RAG 准确率的前提下，减少结构上下文 token 消耗，是本文的核心动机。

Method: 1) 提出 VikingRAG，将语义访问与结构访问紧密结合，支持证据缺口驱动的多轮检索；2) 将多轮检索轨迹物化为"经验边"，供相似查询复用，避免重复多轮探索；3) 引入自适应升级策略，当证据充分时用一轮经验增强检索作答，否则才调用多轮检索。

Result: 在真实数据集上，基础系统 VikingRAG 达到与最先进方法相当的高准确率，而 token 消耗仅为其 11.6%–51.9%；结合检索轨迹复用与自适应升级后，token 成本进一步降至 5.1%–32.5%，同时保持有竞争力的准确率和实用的文档存储性能。

Conclusion: VikingRAG 证明了结构上下文高效的多轮检索与经验复用、自适应升级相结合，可显著降低 RAG 的 token 成本并保持准确率，对新兴的 AI 知识库具有实用价值。

Abstract: State-of-the-art retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) methods exploit document structures to acquire sufficient evidence, but often incur substantial token costs. To reduce structural-context tokens without compromising high RAG accuracy, we present {\sf VikingRAG}, a directory-aware semantic data management system that tightly integrates semantic and structural access to support structural-context-efficient, evidence-gap-driven multi-round retrieval. To further reduce token overhead of multi-round interaction, we materialize agentic multi-round retrieval traces as experience edges, and reuse these edges for similar queries, avoiding repeated multi-round exploration. To additionally reduce token costs when agentic multi-round retrieval is unnecessary, we introduce an adaptive escalation strategy that answers from one-round experience-augmented retrieval when the evidence is sufficient, and invokes agentic multi-round retrieval only otherwise. Experiments on real datasets show that the base system {\sf VikingRAG} matches high accuracy of state-of-the-art methods while consuming only 11.6\%--51.9\% of their tokens. With retrieval-trace reuse and adaptive escalation, token costs drop to 5.1\%--32.5\% while maintaining competitive accuracy and practical document-storage performance, showing the utility of this work for emerging AI knowledge bases.

</details>


### [21] [TimelyRAG: Semantic-Temporal Hybrid Retrieval for Time-Critical Question Answering in Overlapping-Evolving Documents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11572)
*Youngeun Nam,Joeun Kim,Hwanjun Song,Susik Yoon,Jae-Gil Lee,Byung Suk Lee*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出TimelyRAG框架与TimelyQABench基准，通过将时间距离纳入排序，解决重叠演化环境下文档修订导致的检索不可靠问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与RAG在开放域问答中虽已进步，但在文档随修订而演化时仍不可靠。现有时间敏感检索方法只处理"不相交演化"（每次更新为独立快照），而法律、政策、法规常处于"重叠演化"环境：修订覆盖早期条款却保留大部分内容，导致版本间语义高度重叠。

Method: 提出TimelyRAG——一种与检索器无关的框架，将时间距离（temporal distance）纳入排序过程，使查询与版本匹配的文档对齐。同时构建TimelyQABench，这是首个面向法规密集型领域、针对重叠演化挑战的基准。

Result: 实验显示一致性提升，nDCG@10最高提升+28.6%，表明时间推理对于在演化文档上进行可靠问答的重要性。

Conclusion: 时间推理是演化文档可靠问答的关键，TimelyRAG与TimelyQABench为重叠演化环境下的研究提供了框架与评测基础，资源已开源。

Abstract: Although large language models (LLMs) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) have advanced open-domain question answering (QA), they remain unreliable when documents evolve through amendments. Existing time-sensitive retrieval methods address only the disjoint-evolving environment, where each update is an independent snapshot. However, laws, policies, and regulations often operate in overlapping-evolving environments, where amendments override earlier clauses while preserving most content, creating strong semantic overlap across versions. We propose TimelyRAG, a retriever-agnostic framework that incorporates temporal distance into ranking to align queries with version-appropriate documents. We also introduce TimelyQABench, the first benchmark for regulation-heavy domains with overlapping-evolving challenges. Experiments show consistent gains, up to +28.6% in nDCG@10, highlighting the importance of temporal reasoning for reliable QA over evolving documents. All resources are available at https://github.com/kaist-dmlab/TimelyRAG.

</details>


### [22] [FedHUR: Learning Hierarchical Utility-Guided Client Relations for Personalized Federated Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11632)
*Mingzhe Han,Jiahao Liu,Dongsheng Li,Jiankui Zhou,Hansu Gu,Peng Zhang,Ning Gu,Tun Lu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出FedHUR框架，通过层级效用信号引导构建客户端关系，实现个性化联邦推荐聚合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦推荐个性化聚合方法基于预定义参数假设（参数相似性或互补性）构建客户端关系，仅构造单一全局关系，无法刻画用户关系的层级性与多粒度特性；且预定义关系无法直接反映相关客户端聚合后能否提升预测性能。

Method: 以item-item filters为关系构建与聚合对象；先聚合和聚类各客户端本地信息获得全局层级信息；每个客户端基于本地信息与全局层级信息计算层级效用信号，指示哪些协作信息有助于提升其预测；服务器利用这些效用信号检索对该客户端有用的客户端，进行进一步个性化聚合。

Result: 在五个真实数据集上的大量实验表明，FedHUR持续优于现有联邦推荐基线，验证了层级效用引导客户端关系学习的有效性。

Conclusion: FedHUR通过层级效用引导的客户端关系学习，有效解决了现有方法单一全局关系与预定义假设的局限，提升了联邦推荐性能。

Abstract: Federated recommendation enables collaborative model training while keeping user interaction data on local clients. A central problem in federated recommendation is how to aggregate useful information across clients for personalized recommendation. Existing personalized aggregation methods usually construct client relations from predefined parameter-based assumptions, such as parameter similarity or complementarity, and use these relations to determine aggregation weights. However, such methods construct a single global relation, which is insufficient to capture the hierarchical and multi-granularity nature of user relations in recommendation. Moreover, these predefined relations cannot directly reflect whether the related clients can improve prediction performance after aggregation. To address these limitations, we propose FedHUR, a federated recommendation framework for learning hierarchical utility-guided client relations. FedHUR takes item-item filters as the object for relation construction and aggregation. Specifically, it first aggregates and clusters each client's local information to obtain global hierarchical information. Each client computes hierarchical utility signals based on its local information and the global hierarchical information, indicating which collaborative information is useful for improving its prediction. The server uses these utility signals to retrieve clients that are useful to that client for further personalized aggregation. Extensive experiments on five real-world datasets show that FedHUR consistently outperforms existing federated recommendation baselines, demonstrating the effectiveness of hierarchical utility-guided client relation learning. Code is available at https://github.com/Mingzhe-Han/FedHUR.

</details>


### [23] [Your Retriever Already Knows: Distribution-Shape QPP for RAG Retrieval Sufficiency](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11646)
*Matyáš Veselý,Michal Průšek,Jiří Franc*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文比较了三种查询性能预测（QPP）范式（评分特征、内容型LLM评判、混合型）用于RAG检索充分性判断，发现24个非词汇特征（GeneralQPP）在ViDoRe上以2ms/查询达到0.856加权AUROC，远优于本地LLM评判，且在SÚJB数据集上混合方法取得显著优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准RAG流水线在推理时缺乏检索是否成功的可靠信号，在模糊或超范围查询时生成可能产生幻觉。研究动机源于捷克核监管机构部署场景，其中数据敏感性排除了使用第三方LLM API的可能。

Method: 比较三种QPP范式：评分特征、内容型LLM评判、混合型。提出GeneralQPP，包含24个非词汇特征（15个分布形状、5个查询表面、4个全局）。在八个ViDoRe视觉领域（14,514个查询）和SÚJB数据集（1,510个查询，500个合成对抗样本）上评估，采用Leave-One-Domain-Out等方法。

Result: GeneralQPP在ViDoRe上加权平均AUROC为0.856，2ms/查询，优于经典QPP文献池（0.835）和本地Qwen3.5 LLM评判（0.649），约快3000倍。混合方法在ViDoRe上匹配S1（0.863），在SÚJB上显著领先（Hit@5时AUROC 0.911，对抗检测0.954）。数据集间排名一致（Spearman ρ=0.90）。LODO下S1降至0.706，13特征子集S1-Lean恢复至0.719（比文献池高0.032）。

Conclusion: 基于分数特征的高效非词汇QPP方法在检索充分性判断上优于LLM评判，兼具速度与成本优势。混合方法在特定数据集上有增益但需LLM延迟代价。S1-Lean特征子集提升了跨域泛化能力。

Abstract: Standard Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines often provide no reliable inference-time signal of whether retrieval succeeded; on ambiguous or out-of-scope queries, generation may then hallucinate. Motivated by a Czech nuclear-regulator deployment where data sensitivity precludes third-party LLM APIs, we compare three Query Performance Prediction (QPP) paradigms for retrieval sufficiency in RAG: score-based features, a content-based LLM judge, and a hybrid. On the eight ViDoRe vision domains (14,514 queries), our 24 non-lexical features (GeneralQPP; 15 distribution-shape, 5 query-surface, 4 global) reach a weighted-average AUROC of 0.856 at 2 ms per query, ahead of a classic-QPP literature pool (Classic Full, 0.835) and well above a local Qwen3.5 LLM judge (0.649, +0.207 gap; $\sim$3000$\times$ faster and cheaper per query). Adding the LLM judgment as one feature (hybrid) matches S1 on ViDoRe (0.863) but gains a statistically significant edge on SÚJB (AUROC 0.911 at Hit@5, adversarial-detection 0.954; 1,510 queries, 500 synthetic adversarial), at LLM latency. Rankings agree across datasets (Spearman $ρ= 0.90$). Under Leave-One-Domain-Out, S1 drops to 0.706; a 13-feature LODO-stepwise subset (S1-Lean) recovers to 0.719 (+0.032 over the literature pool).

</details>


### [24] [Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings For Visual Document Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11808)
*Mohamed Eltahir,Talal Aloushan,Rose Khairoalsendi,Jana Shata,Mohammed Alhassan,Leen Alrehaili,Tanveer Hussain,Naeemullah Khan*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文提出GLIE，一种基于几何先验的生成式后期交互嵌入压缩方法：每页仅存k<<N个向量作为轻量索引，查询时仅用这些向量检索，再对Top候选解码重建全部N个向量做精确重排。在ViDoRe v1上，每页4向量时GLIE保留约80%的nDCG@5（最优基线70%），且训练成本极低（415K参数、千页数据、3 GPU分钟）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后期交互检索在视觉文档搜索中精度最优，但存储代价高（每页约1000个向量）。现有压缩方法仅保留子集或局部平均，在激进存储预算下精度急剧下降，而替代方案需重训练编码器。作者希望在不重训编码器的前提下，实现高压缩比且精度损失小的存储高效检索。

Method: 作者先在三种编码器上分析发现两个一致性几何性质：向量精确位于单位球面上，且集中在一个内在维度为5–6的流形附近。基于此提出两点：1）标准k-means质心落在球内，导致MaxSim分数系统性低估，将质心归一化到球面可免费提升最多+0.093 nDCG@5；2）由于页面流形自由度低，可由少量向量重建全部向量。据此提出GLIE：每页学习k<<N个向量（由归一化质心初始化），既作轻量索引又作重建基。查询时仅在k个向量上检索，解码器只对Top候选扩展回全部N个向量进行精确重排。

Result: 在ViDoRe v1上，每页4向量时GLIE保留约80%未压缩系统的nDCG@5，而最优事后压缩方法仅70%。该结果来自一个415K参数网络，在仅1000个训练页面上训练不到3 GPU分钟。在同等训练预算下，微调编码器甚至达不到GLIE的无训练阶段，且GLIE完整系统在所有预算下均优于微调编码器。上述规律在第二种编码器和ViDoRe v2上同样成立。

Conclusion: GLIE通过按需重建证据而非采样证据，开辟了存储高效检索的新方向，其中解码器是主要的设计空间。其核心贡献在于揭示后期交互嵌入的低维球面几何，并据此实现无需编码器重训、可即插即用的高压缩检索方案。

Abstract: Late-interaction retrieval is the state-of-the-art for visual document search, but it pays for its accuracy in storage. Existing compression methods retain a subset or local average of the N~1,000 vectors per page. Under aggressive storage budgets, however, these methods degrade sharply, and alternatives require retraining the encoder. Investigating this degradation across three encoders, we found two consistent properties: the vectors lie exactly on the unit sphere and concentrate near a manifold of intrinsic dimension five to six. This geometry yields two insights. First, standard k-means centroids fall inside the sphere, causing systematic underestimation of MaxSim scores. Normalizing them to the surface is a free correction worth up to +0.093 nDCG@5 over raw centroids. Second, because the page manifold has few degrees of freedom, the full set of vectors can be regenerated from only a few. To this end, we introduce Generative Late-Interaction Embeddings (GLIE): k << N vectors per page learned from the normalized centroids to serve as both a lightweight index and a basis for regenerating the page's full embedding set. At query time, search runs exclusively on these k vectors, and a decoder expands only the top candidates back to all N vectors for exact rescoring. At four vectors per page on ViDoRe v1, GLIE retains nearly 80% of the uncompressed system's nDCG@5, against 70% for the best prior post-hoc method. These results use a 415K-parameter network fitted in under three GPU-minutes on just a thousand training pages. At a matched training budget, fine-tuning the encoder does not reach even the training-free stage of GLIE, and the full system beats it at every budget. These patterns hold across a second encoder and ViDoRe v2. By reconstructing evidence on demand rather than sampling it, GLIE opens a new axis for storage-efficient retrieval, with the decoder as its main design surface.

</details>

### [25] [When Passing Tests Hides Vulnerabilities: An Empirical Study of Silent Failures in Agentic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10548)
*Wenji Bai,Muhammad Waseem,Zeeshan Rasheed,Jaakko Peltonen,Pekka Abrahamsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究通过1030条执行轨迹的实证分析，揭示LLM智能体代码修复中存在通过语法与功能验证却保留或引入漏洞的"静默失败"，并将其归纳为遗漏、引入、不足三大类及十种细粒度代码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体自动代码修复受到广泛关注，但很少有人关注那些通过语法和功能验证、却仍保留或引入安全漏洞的补丁，即"静默失败"。研究旨在系统识别并归类这类失败现象。

Method: 基于七个智能体框架与GPT-4o-mini在两个安全数据集（SecurityEval与CVEfixes）上产生的1030条有效执行轨迹开展实证研究，经三轮定性编码与人工验证，确认了170个静默失败案例。

Result: 识别出三大失败类别：遗漏（48.2%）、引入（30.6%）、不足（21.2%），并细分为十种失败代码；现有测试通过式评估与LLM评审角色均不足以暴露或拦截这些失败；不同框架出现相似不安全方案，提示存在共享的模型、提示或任务层面影响，且单智能体与多智能体系统呈现不同的失败特征。

Conclusion: 该研究有助于研究者和实践者改进LLM智能体代码修复的评估方式，并开发超越功能正确性、覆盖所有生成产物的针对性验证方法。

Abstract: LLM-based agents for automated code repair have received significant attention in recent years from both research and software engineering practice perspectives. However, limited attention has been paid to patches that pass syntactic and functional verification but still retain or introduce security vulnerabilities. The aim of this research is to systematically identify and categorize such silent failures in LLM-based agentic code repair.
  We conducted an empirical study using 1,030 valid execution traces produced by seven agent frameworks with GPT-4o-mini across two security-focused datasets, SecurityEval and CVEfixes. Through three iterations of qualitative coding and manual verification, 170 confirmed silent failures were identified. The key results are: (i) Three main categories of silent failures were identified: Omission, Introduction, and Inadequacy. Omission accounts for 48.2% of the confirmed failures, Introduction for 30.6%, and Inadequacy for 21.2%. (ii) Ten fine-grained failure codes were classified under these three categories, showing how agents omit required security controls, apply incomplete defenses, or introduce new vulnerabilities during repair. (iii) Current test-passing evaluation and LLM-based reviewer roles were insufficient to expose or intercept these failures in the confirmed cases. (iv) Similar insecure solutions appeared across different frameworks, suggesting possible shared model-, prompt-, or task-level influences, while single-agent and multi-agent systems showed different failure profiles.
  The results of this study will assist researchers and practitioners in improving the evaluation of LLM-based agentic code repair and developing targeted verification methods that go beyond functional correctness and cover all generated artifacts.

</details>


### [26] [Optimizing AI Inference Across the Deployment Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10550)
*Tejinder Singh,John Pflueger,Jeebak Mitra,Robert Lincourt,Mitchell Markow,Bhavesh A. Patel*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出跨部署栈的推理优化统一分析框架,含三层分类法、多目标优化形式化与证据协议,强调跨层交互决定部署性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在不可比条件下报告延迟与吞吐,难以支撑部署决策;性能并非仅由模型架构决定,而受压缩、编译器变换与服务策略的交互影响。

Method: 提出三层分类法(模型级量化/剪枝/蒸馏、编译器图融合/布局优化/内核自动调优、系统动态批处理/准入控制/内存分层);将部署形式化为在精度、延迟、吞吐、内存与能耗上的约束多目标优化,分析具Pareto单调性与尺度不变性的排序泛函;用Roofline模型刻画带宽层级约束,用排队模型解释负载下响应时间放大;提出区分实测/推导/解析声明的证据协议。

Result: 综合边缘平台(Jetson AGX Orin与五个推理框架)、数据中心GPU(A100、H100与三个LLM服务引擎)及Llama-3.1系列量化研究,显示部署结果由单层分析无法预测的跨层交互主导。

Conclusion: 给出约束感知的选择流程,并指出编译器-服务协同优化、跨硬件性能预测与标准化能耗报告等开放问题。

Abstract: AI deployment performance is shaped not by model architecture alone, but by interactions among compression, compiler transformations, and serving policies. Published benchmarks often report latency and throughput under incomparable conditions, limiting their use for deployment decisions. This paper presents a unified analytical treatment of inference optimization across the deployment stack. We introduce a three-layer taxonomy covering model-level techniques such as quantization, pruning, and distillation; compiler transformations such as graph fusion, layout optimization, and kernel autotuning; and system policies such as dynamic batching, admission control, and memory tiering. We formulate deployment as a constrained multi-objective optimization problem over accuracy, latency, throughput, memory footprint, and energy, and analyze a deployment-ranking functional with Pareto monotonicity and scale invariance. Roofline models show how memory-bandwidth hierarchies bound performance across precision regimes, while queuing models explain how service-time changes amplify response time under load. To improve comparability, we propose an evidence protocol that separates measured, derived, and analytical claims; limits numerical comparison to within-paper results; and requires reporting of hardware, software versions, batch semantics, and thermal state. We synthesize evidence from edge platforms, including Jetson AGX Orin and five inference frameworks; data center GPUs, including A100 and H100 with three LLM serving engines; and quantization studies across the Llama-3.1 family. The synthesis shows that deployment outcomes are governed by cross-layer interactions that no single-layer analysis can predict. We conclude with a constraint-aware selection procedure and open problems in compiler-serving co-optimization, cross-hardware performance prediction, and standardized energy reporting.

</details>


### [27] [ReqEvolve: User-Oriented Software Self-Evolution through Automatic Requirement Interpretation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10590)
*Md Asif Iqbal Fahim,Alessio Ferrari*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: ReqEvolve是一个运行时代码生成系统，通过接受高层用户请求，结合自动需求工程与测试驱动开发，实现用户驱动的软件自演化，在72个演化案例中达到89.2%的Pass@1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件自演化虽能让系统在执行期间自主扩展和重构能力，但新功能请求往往来自终端用户，且很少以技术术语表达。开发者必须先将用户需求翻译为技术规格，系统才能演化，这延迟了功能的早期验证，用户无法立即观察生成的行为。

Method: 提出ReqEvolve运行时代码生成系统，支持用户驱动的自演化，可直接接受高层用户请求。系统集成自动需求工程（RE）与测试驱动开发（TDD），通过澄清、规格分解、测试生成和运行时集成四个环节，将用户请求转化为可执行功能。

Result: 在18个项目的72个软件演化案例上进行评估，对比SpecFix（以需求工程为重点的代码生成方法）和本系统的消融变体。ReqEvolve达到89.2%的Pass@1，比SpecFix高18.8%（p < 0.01, r = 0.79，大效应），比消融基线高32.6%（p < 0.001, r = 0.88，大效应）。

Conclusion: 结果初步证明，用户驱动的自演化是一种可行范式，能够从用户请求出发自主扩展软件能力，从而在开发者验证之前加速需求验证。

Abstract: The paradigm of software self-evolution enables systems to autonomously extend and reconfigure their own capabilities during execution in response to technical specifications. Yet requests for new functionality often originate from end users and are rarely expressed in technical terms. As a result, developers must translate user needs into technical specifications before the system can evolve, delaying early validation of the requested functionality by preventing users from immediately observing the resulting behaviour. To address this gap, we present ReqEvolve, a runtime code generation system that enables user-driven self-evolution by accepting high-level user requests. The system integrates automatic requirements engineering (RE) and test-driven development (TDD) to transform these requests into executable functionality through clarification, specification decomposition, test generation, and runtime integration. We evaluate ReqEvolve on 72 software evolution cases across 18 projects against two baselines: SpecFix, an RE-focused code generation approach, and an ablation variant of our system. ReqEvolve achieves 89.2% Pass@1, outperforming SpecFix by 18.8% (p < 0.01, r = 0.79, large effect) and the ablation baseline by 32.6% (p < 0.001, r = 0.88, large effect). These results provide initial evidence that user-driven self-evolution is a viable paradigm for autonomously extending software capabilities from user requests, thereby accelerating requirements validation prior to developer verification.

</details>


### [28] [Generative AI for trustworthy systems - Towards a health check model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10595)
*Jan Bosch,Rick Kazman,Henry Muccini,Helena Holmström Olsson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出"可信自主性健康检查模型"，一个多维诊断工具，用八个维度与四种信任范式刻画组织如何为GenAI辅助软件工程建立信任，强调维度对齐而非单一成熟度进阶。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI在软件密集型系统中的采纳速度很快，但现有分析工具主要是单维度成熟度模型，将重要的配置性差异压缩为单一递进轴，无法刻画组织间在信任建立方式上的结构性差异。

Method: 基于对电信、汽车、国防、航空、银行、能源、政府和软件服务等领域十八位资深从业者的归纳式访谈研究，构建模型：八个实证维度分为系统层（代理权限范围、保障机制、数据可信性、架构围堵、可追溯性与可理解性）和组织层（治理、人工监督姿态、劳动力能力可持续性），每维采用五级序数量表；并叠加运营、工程、统计与围堵四类信任范式作为横切视角。

Result: 模型为诊断性而非规范性工具，支持跨组织比较、揭示配置权衡，并将组织定位于共享空间而不强加单一演进路径；高层级并非天然更优。由此形式化出"对齐假设"：有效可信性受限于代理权限范围与支撑该范围的各维度之间的匹配程度，错配会导致病态风险或不必要的摩擦。

Conclusion: 论文讨论了实践者如何应用该模型，并提出后续实证验证的方向，将可信性理解为领域与信任范式相关的多维对齐问题，而非成熟度等级的高低。

Abstract: The adoption of generative AI in software-intensive systems is proceeding rapidly, but current analytical instruments - principally unidimensional maturity models - compress important configurational variation into a single progressive axis. Drawing on an inductive interview study of eighteen senior practitioners across telecommunications, automotive, defence, aviation, banking, energy, government, and enterprise software services, this paper presents the Trustworthy Autonomy Health Check Model: a structured, multidimensional instrument for characterizing how organizations establish trust in GenAI-assisted software engineering. The model organizes eight empirically grounded dimensions into a system layer (Scope of Agent Authority, Assurance Mechanisms, Data Trustworthiness, Architectural Containment, Traceability & Comprehensibility) and an organizational layer (Governance, Human Oversight Posture, Workforce Capability Sustainability), each on a five-level ordinal scale. A cross-cutting overlay of four trust paradigms - operational, engineering, statistical, and containment-based - captures how trust is established, complementing the dimensions capturing what must be trustworthy. The model is diagnostic rather than prescriptive: it supports cross-organizational comparison, surfaces configurational trade-offs, and locates an organization in a shared space without imposing a single progression path. Higher levels are not inherently better; the goal is alignment across dimensions appropriate to the organization's domain and chosen trust paradigm. This is formalized as the alignment hypothesis: effective trustworthiness is constrained by the fit between the scope of agent authority and the dimensions enabling it, with misalignment producing pathological risk or unnecessary friction. We discuss how practitioners can apply the model and outline directions for empirical validation.

</details>


### [29] [On the Relation between Code Quality and Machine Learning Performance: A Large-scale Empirical Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10610)
*Marius Mignard,Steven Costiou,Anne Etien*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对265,363个Kaggle笔记本的大规模实证研究显示：通用Python代码质量与ML性能无关，而ML特定规则违规与性能存在持续的小幅负相关；流行度无法指示代码质量或性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算笔记本是机器学习开发的标准环境，但社区普遍将模型性能视为首要指标，代码质量被视为次要问题。这种优先级依赖一个基本未经检验的假设：代码质量与ML性能无关。此外，从业者常复用通过社交信号（流行度、作者专业度）筛选的笔记本代码，而这些信号作为质量代理的可靠性从未被评估。

Method: 对提交至Kaggle竞赛的265,363个Python笔记本进行大规模实证研究。使用两种静态分析工具评估代码质量：Pylint（捕捉通用Python代码质量）和配置了34条面向数据科学与ML特定实践规则的SonarQube。

Result: 代码质量与性能的关系取决于所考虑的质量概念。通用Python代码质量与ML性能解耦，在所有观察中呈现可忽略或不显著的关联；相反，ML特定违规与性能呈现一致的小幅负相关，且在所有观察中持续存在。笔记本流行度不能提供关于代码质量或性能的信息。代码专业度对质量或性能无信息价值，但竞赛专业度与更好的性能、更少的ML特定违规以及略多的Python错误和重构违规相关。

Conclusion: 代码质量与ML性能的关系并非单一维度：通用代码质量与性能无关，而ML特定实践违规与性能存在稳定的负相关。流行度和作者专业度作为质量代理并不可靠，竞赛专业度虽与性能相关但同时也伴随略多的通用代码问题。

Abstract: Context: Computational notebooks are the standard environment for machine learning (ML) development. Within the ML community, model performance is often the primary considered metric, and code quality is treated as a secondary concern. This prioritization relies on a largely untested assumption that code quality and ML performance are unrelated. Practitioners also reuse existing code that may come from notebooks selected through social signals (popularity, author expertise) whose reliability as quality proxies has never been assessed. Objective: We empirically investigated the relationship between code quality and ML performance in notebooks, and evaluated whether popularity and author expertise give indication on code quality or performance. Method: We conducted a large-scale empirical study of 265,363 Python notebooks submitted to Kaggle competitions. We assessed code quality with two static analysis tools: Pylint, capturing general Python code quality, and SonarQube, configured with a profile of 34 rules targeting data-science and ML-specific practices. Results: The relationship between code quality and performance depends on the notion of quality considered. General Python code quality is decoupled from ML performance, showing negligible or non-significant correlations across all observations. In contrast, ML-specific violations exhibit a consistent, small negative association with performance that persists across all observations. The popularity of a notebook does not give information on the code quality or performance. Code expertise provides no information on quality or performance, but competition expertise correlates with better performance, fewer ML-specific violations, and slightly more Python errors and refactoring violations.

</details>


### [30] [AI Safety: Not Optional, Not Later](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10630)
*Qinghua Lu,Yoshua Bengio*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种"安全源于设计"的保障架构，将模型层监督（如 Scientist AI）与系统层控制（脚手架与执行框架管控、独立验证、监测、证据基础设施）相结合，并由治理机制支撑问责与证据互操作。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实事件表明，AI 安全失效往往并非发生在单一层面，而是跨多个层级出现。因此仅依赖单一层面的防护不足以应对系统性风险，需要一种覆盖模型、系统与治理多个层次的整合性保障方案。

Method: 提出"安全源于设计"（safety-by-design）的保障架构，包含三个维度：（1）模型层监督，例如 Scientist AI；（2）系统层控制，覆盖脚手架（scaffolds）与执行框架（harnesses）、独立验证、监测以及证据基础设施；（3）治理支撑，用于保障问责机制与证据的互操作性。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或量化评估，主要贡献为架构性框架的提出与层次划分。

Conclusion: AI 安全保障应被视为跨层级的系统工程：将模型层监督、系统层控制与治理机制整合于统一架构中，并通过证据基础设施与互操作性支撑问责，从而更有效地应对多层次的安全失效。

Abstract: Incidents show that AI safety failures often arise across multiple layers. We present a safety-by-design assurance architecture combining model-level supervision, such as Scientist AI, with system-level controls over scaffolds and harnesses, independent verification, monitoring, and evidence infrastructure, supported by governance for accountability and evidence interoperability.

</details>


### [31] [Numbat: Building and Verifying a Self-Contained Machine-Learning Stack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10632)
*Thang Tran,Lan Dang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文构建并验证了 numbat：一个用单一通用语言（Zig）编写、无第三方运行时依赖的机器学习栈，跨越张量计算、自动微分、混合精度、多GPU训练等，并通过以参考实现为可执行规范的五级验证协议（轨迹级差分预言机）发现十处静默配方分歧。验收实验中，25.9M参数YOLOv8m类检测器在COCO 2017上训练500轮，导出权重以参考栈验证器评分达0.4956 mAP50-95（已发布端点0.502），单GPU步时持平。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前机器学习系统几乎都建立在少数大型Python编排框架之上，继承了其工程成本：数百个版本耦合包的环境、独立的部署导出工具链，以及研究语言与产品发布语言之间的割裂。作者希望用单一通用语言构建无第三方运行时依赖的完整栈来消除这些成本。

Method: 用Zig实现覆盖张量计算、自动微分、神经网络模块、混合精度、多GPU训练、数据加载与监控的完整栈；通过稳定且加法式版本化的C ABI（超过1400个入口点）暴露SDK，并提供六种语言绑定；将临床领域平面的监管要求编码为可执行验收门。验证方面，把广泛使用的参考实现作为可执行规范，在五个层级上进行验证，从算子梯度检查到针对同机参考运行的自动化轨迹门。

Result: 验证协议揭示十处静默配方分歧，并记录了其机制与症状。验收测试中，从随机初始化在COCO 2017上按完整500轮计划训练25.9M参数YOLOv8m类检测器，导出权重在官方协议下由参考栈自身验证器评分为0.4956 mAP50-95（已发布端点0.502），相同硬件上单GPU步时持平。权重、逐轮指标与完整运行清单均已发布。

Conclusion: numbat证明可以用单一通用语言、无第三方运行时依赖构建覆盖训练到部署的完整机器学习栈，并通过轨迹级差分验证协议达到与参考实现相当的精度与性能；该协议将作为轨迹级差分预言机在配套研究中形式化，权重与运行产物已公开。

Abstract: Machine-learning systems are built almost exclusively on a few large Python-orchestrated frameworks, and they inherit those stacks' engineering costs: environments of hundreds of version-coupled packages, separate export toolchains for deployment, and the split between the language research is written in and the language products ship in. We report on the construction and verification of numbat, a machine-learning stack written in one general-purpose language (Zig) with no third-party runtime dependencies. The stack spans tensor computation, automatic differentiation, neural-network modules, mixed precision, multi-GPU training, data loading and monitoring; an SDK exposes it behind a stable, additively versioned C ABI of over 1,400 entry points, with bindings for six languages; and its clinical domain planes encode regulatory requirements as executable acceptance gates rather than documentation. Verifying such a stack is the harder half of building it: a defective training run rarely fails, it converges quietly to a slightly worse model. We treat a widely used reference implementation as an executable specification and verify against it at five levels, from operator gradient checks to an automated trajectory gate against a same-machine reference run - the arrangement our companion study formalizes as a trajectory-level differential oracle. The protocol surfaced ten silent recipe divergences, which we catalog with mechanisms and symptoms. As the acceptance test, we train a 25.9M-parameter detector of the YOLOv8m class from random initialization on COCO 2017 for the full 500-epoch schedule: the exported weights score 0.4956 mAP50-95 under the official protocol, scored by the reference stack's own validator (published endpoint 0.502), with single-GPU step time at parity on identical hardware. Weights, per-epoch metrics and the full run manifest are released.

</details>
