*Azzeddine Ihsine,Sara Ihsine*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究D-POAF决策实践中五个量化算子（BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP）的行为特性，通过受控合成实验验证其在证据加权的人机协同优先级排序中的可靠性与优化边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI原生软件工程中优先级决策同时融合了人类判断、历史类比、参数估计与AI生成预测，核心难题在于如何对异构估计、不确定性、战略参数、依赖关系与有限容量进行可审查、可重校准的治理。

Method: 针对BVS、ERS、PVS、CCS、ODP五个算子，在已知潜在变量与显式误差过程下开展受控合成实验；包含BVS权重扰动、可靠性加权努力聚合、估计器漂移下的自适应加权、模型集体分歧检测，以及800个依赖约束的组合实例测试。

Result: 中等BVS权重扰动下全局排序保持稳定（Spearman中位数0.986），但更广战略变化使前10%重叠降至0.788；可靠性加权努力聚合MAE为0.616，较最佳单一估计器（1.073）降低42.6%，并优于简单均值（0.638）；估计器漂移下自适应加权RMSE降低4.5%；集体分歧检测高误差估计ROC-AUC达0.906；组合实例中价值-努力比平均目标比0.962、ODP距离0.953，bootstrap区间确认其小幅系统性优势。

Conclusion: 研究为证据加权的人机协同优先级排序建立了量化基础，并界定了最短路径ODP公式与一般发布组合选择之间的优化边界。

Abstract: AI-native software engineering increasingly combines human judgment, historical analogy, parametric estimation, and AI-generated forecasts inside the same prioritization decision. The resulting problem is not merely how to rank candidate work, but how to govern heterogeneous estimates, uncertainty, strategic parameters, dependencies, and limited capacity in a way that remains inspectable and recalibratable. We study five quantitative operators used in the D-POAF decision practice: Business Value Score (BVS), Effort and Risk Score (ERS), Prioritization Value Score (PVS), Collective Calibration Score (CCS), and Optimal Development Path (ODP). Controlled synthetic experiments characterize their behavior under known latent variables and explicit error processes. Moderate BVS-weight perturbations preserved global rankings (median Spearman 0.986), while broader strategic changes reduced top-10% overlap to 0.788. Reliability-weighted effort aggregation achieved MAE 0.616, a 42.6% reduction relative to the best individual estimator (MAE 1.073), and outperformed a simple mean (MAE 0.638). Under estimator drift, adaptive reliability weighting reduced average RMSE by 4.5% relative to equal weighting. Model-collective divergence detected the highest-error priority estimates with ROC-AUC 0.906. In 800 dependency-constrained portfolio instances, value-to-effort achieved mean objective ratio 0.962 and ODP distance 0.953 against the exact optimum; bootstrap intervals confirm a small but systematic advantage for value-to-effort under the declared objective. These results establish a quantitative basis for evidence-weighted human-AI prioritization and define the optimization boundary between shortest-path ODP formulations and general release-portfolio selection.

</details>


### [33] [LLMVul: A Vulnerability-Labeled Dataset of LLM-Generated C/C++ Functions from Real Production Repositories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10945)
*Mohammad Farhad,Shuvalaxmi Dass*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出LLMVul——一个从真实生产仓库挖掘的LLM生成C/C++函数漏洞标注数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有漏洞数据集多聚焦人工编写代码或受控提示环境，难以研究真实软件项目中LLM生成代码的安全弱点。

Method: 从GitHub挖掘4年（2022.11.13–2026.9.3）AI辅助开发活动，利用提交元数据与AI作者证据等溯源信号，过滤去重后得到21430个C/C++函数；采用静态分析与模式方法集成标注漏洞，并做独立人工标注验证。

Result: 数据集含来自226个仓库的21430个唯一函数，其中1540个集成判定为漏洞函数，覆盖17类CWE；人工标注一致性Cohen's kappa为0.79。

Conclusion: LLMVul提供比现有基准更多真实LLM生成漏洞函数，保留代码标签与生成溯源元数据，支持漏洞检测、安全评估与AI辅助开发模式分析的可复现研究，已公开。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate and assist with software development, yet existing vulnerability datasets largely focus on human-written code or controlled prompting environments. This limits the ability to study security weaknesses in LLM-generated code as it appears in real-world software projects. We present LLMVul, a vulnerability-labeled dataset of LLM-generated C/C++ functions mined from real production repositories. We mine AI-assisted development activity from GitHub over a 4 year period, from November 13, 2022 to September 3, 2026, using provenance signals such as commit metadata and AI-related authorship evidence. After filtering and deduplication, LLMVul contains 21,430 unique C/C++ functions from 226 repositories, together with repository, commit, function, provenance, and AI-tool metadata. We establish vulnerability labels using an ensemble of complementary static-analysis and pattern-based techniques and assign Common Weakness Enumeration (CWE) categories to confirmed vulnerable functions. To assess labeling reliability, we additionally conduct independent manual annotation and measure inter-rater agreement using Cohen's kappa ($k=0.79$). LLMVul contains 1,540 ensemble-vulnerable functions spanning 17 unique CWE categories, providing substantially more real-world LLM-generated vulnerable C/C++ functions than existing vulnerability-oriented LLM code benchmarks. By preserving both code-level vulnerability labels and generation/provenance metadata, LLMVul enables reproducible research on vulnerability detection, security evaluation of LLM-generated code, and analysis of vulnerability patterns in AI-assisted software development. The LLMVul dataset is publicly available at https://doi.org/10.5281/zenodo.22668216.

</details>


### [34] [What a Random Draw from the MCP Registry Contains, and What Tool-Use Benchmarks Contain Instead](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10962)
*Haseeb Mohammed Afsar*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对24,135个MCP服务器注册表进行概率抽样，实测400个npm/stdio服务器的真实可用性与工具描述质量，发现真实生态的启动成功率、安全注解完整度和描述去重特性均与文献中基于精选样本的结论存在显著差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MCP服务器生态研究普遍采用参考集、热门榜单、人工筛选框架或"修复后可用"的流水线来构造样本，这类抽样方式隐性地只保留了能正常运行的服务器，导致对生态真实状况（如启动失败率、注解缺失率、描述重复度）的估计被系统性高估或美化。作者希望报告一个未经修复的概率样本究竟包含什么。

Method: 从24,135个服务器的注册表普查中，以公开随机种子抽取400个npm/stdio服务器，逐个通过网络探测其行为。将结果与同一测量工具下的人工精选框架对比；统计initialize握手成功率、失败原因分布、JSON Schema合规性、可选安全注解省略率。随后用统一方法将该样本广告的工具描述与BFCL v4、UltraTool两个工具使用基准语料对比，计算余弦相似度阈值下的近似重复率（含跨作者重复与服务器内重复），并统计原始数据集中"名称+描述"完全重复的行占比。

Result: 仅48.8%的服务器完成initialize握手，人工精选框架为66.7%；主要失败原因是服务器根本无法启动（37.5%），而非缺少凭证（13.3%）。195个可运行服务器在2,766个广告工具上零致命JSON Schema违规，硬性合规性完全达标。可选安全注解是可变成分：随机抽样工具级省略率58.8%，精选框架为41.5%，说明筛选同样美化了该指标。与基准语料对比：真实MCP工具在余弦0.70下近似重复率2.8%且全部发生在单一服务器内部，跨作者重复在各阈值下均为0.0%；BFCL v4为16.7%（其中16.4个百分点来自独立呈现的任务之间），UltraTool仅0.3%（比真实工具更干净），说明重复问题是BFCL的特性而非合成语料的普遍属性。原始BFCL有68.8%、UltraTool有85.6%的行是名称加描述的完全重复，而真实MCP仅0.4%。

Conclusion: 基于精选样本的MCP生态研究系统性高估了服务器可用性和注解完整度，真实概率样本显示近半服务器无法启动、安全注解大面积缺失。同时，主流工具使用基准语料存在严重重复且未经全局去重，任何在其上计算的统计量实际测量的是重复而非工具本身；跨作者工具描述重复在真实生态中并不存在。所有数字可通过已发布的脚本和公开种子复现。

Abstract: Studies of the Model Context Protocol (MCP) server ecosystem draw their samples in ways that quietly select for servers that work: reference sets, popularity lists, hand-curated frames, or pipelines that repair a server until it starts. We report what an unrepaired probability sample actually contains. From a 24,135-server registry census we draw 400 npm/stdio servers with a published seed and probe each one over the wire. Only 48.8% complete an initialize handshake, against 66.7% for a hand-curated frame measured with the same instrument, and the dominant failure is not missing credentials (13.3%) but servers that never start at all (37.5%). Among the 195 that do run, hard conformance is total: zero fatal JSON Schema violations across 2,766 advertised tools. Optional safety annotations are the real variance, and the tool-level omission rate on a random draw is 58.8% against 41.5% on the curated frame, so curation flatters this figure too. We then compare the tool descriptions these servers advertise against two tool-use benchmark corpora using one method held constant. Real MCP tools show 2.8% near-duplication at cosine 0.70, and all of it lies within single servers: cross-author near-duplication is 0.0% at every threshold tested. BFCL v4 shows 16.7%, of which 16.4 points lie between independently presented tasks. UltraTool shows 0.3%, cleaner than real tools, so this is a property of BFCL and not of synthetic corpora as a class. Separately, 68.8% of raw BFCL rows and 85.6% of raw UltraTool rows are exact name-plus-description repeats, against 0.4% for real MCP, so any statistic computed over these releases without global deduplication measures repetition rather than tools. All figures regenerate from released scripts and a published seed.

</details>


### [35] [Engineering Reliable Commit Gates for Agentic AI: Cost-Aware Verification Portfolios under Common-Mode Data Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10969)
*Zihao Zheng,Baichuan Li,Junyi Yao,Jiayu Long*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 VP-CONTROL 运行时保障设计与确定性基准，用于成本感知的提交门控，通过 2x2 实验区分验证器模型多样性与证据来源多样性，并揭示来源多样性远优于模型多样性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统会执行改变状态的动作，但额外验证器可能继承相同的上游故障，因此需要研究如何在成本约束下设计可靠的提交门控。

Method: 构建 VP-CONTROL 确定性基准，48 个任务模板生成六大故障机制下的 2,880 个场景；采用固定调用次数的 2x2 实验分离验证器模型多样性与证据来源多样性；使用仅依赖部署可观测元数据的组合式控制器选择验证方案；并开展预注册的实时 HTTP/SQLite 研究，测试并发写入与响应丢失场景下的原子守卫与幂等请求标识。

Result: 在冻结提案上，共享证据的跨模型投票批准了 62.9% 的不安全提案，而独立来源仅 22.9%；来源效应达 40.9 个百分点，模型多样性为 11.3。组合控制器在锁定测试上实现 1.9% 不安全执行与 38.2% 自动安全覆盖；同预算组合优于固定验证策略。但迁移性有限：未见故障族风险 16-26%，FinQA 检查未能复现来源效应。实时研究中门后竞态击败仅验证器门控，事务性部分守卫仅防止已覆盖失败，完整原子守卫在 216 个回合中无不安全效应，幂等请求标识可防止响应丢失后的重复效应。

Conclusion: 结果支持显式证据谱系、成本感知选择与提交时强制执行，同时暴露近似校准与本地工具泛化的局限。

Abstract: Agentic systems commit state-changing actions, but additional verifiers can inherit the same upstream fault. We present VP-CONTROL, a runtime-assurance design and deterministic benchmark for cost-aware commit gates. Its 48 task templates yield 2,880 scenarios across six fault regimes. A fixed-call 2 x 2 experiment separates verifier-model diversity from evidence-source diversity. On frozen proposals from two local actor families, a cross-model vote over shared evidence approves 62.9% of unsafe proposals, versus 22.9% with an independent source. The source effect is 40.9 percentage points, compared with 11.3 for model diversity. A portfolio controller selects verification plans using only deployment-observable metadata. Approximate cluster-adjusted calibration at a nominal 5% per-task target yields 1.9% unsafe execution and 38.2% automated safe coverage on the locked test. Matched-budget portfolios also improve on fixed verification policies. Transfer remains conditional: unseen fault families yield 16-26% risk, and a FinQA check fails to reproduce the source effect with the tested small verifiers. A preregistered live HTTP/SQLite study tests concurrent writes and lost responses. After-check races defeat verifier-only gates; transactional partial guards prevent only covered failures, while a full atomic guard records no unsafe effects across 216 episodes. Idempotent request identifiers prevent duplicate effects after lost responses. The results motivate explicit evidence lineage, cost-aware selection, and commit-time enforcement, while exposing the limits of approximate calibration and local-tool generalization.

</details>


### [36] [RCL: A Retrieval-Confidence Layer for Detecting Insufficient Context in Enterprise Retrieval-Augmented Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11023)
*Chandra Mohan Ravuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 RCL（Retrieval-Confidence Layer），一种插在检索与生成之间的轻量模块，用调用图结构覆盖率与新颖度分数，在生成前判断私有代码库检索是否充分，并在置信度不足时触发补充检索或转人工审核。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG 代码生成在公开仓库上因模型参数化知识可弥补检索不足而表现良好，但在企业私有代码库中失效：私有 API、内部框架和未文档化的团队约定完全不在预训练分布内。已有工作显示即使完美检索也无法消除错误，失败点位于下游的 API 使用；而基于内部置信度的门控检索仅用于开放域问答。二者均未回答"生成开始前检索本身对私有代码查询是否结构充分"这一问题。

Method: 提出 RCL 模块，置于检索与生成之间，包含两个评分函数：一是从调用图导出的结构覆盖率分数，二是估计查询对模型先验之外知识依赖程度的新颖度分数。当置信度低于标定阈值时，RCL 触发有针对性的补充检索，或标记输出以供人工审查，而非在不完整上下文下静默生成。此外，作者形式化评分函数并设计评测方法：通过向开源 Java 仓库注入合成内部 API，构建私有代码基准，模拟企业条件而不使用专有代码。

Result: 论文报告了 RCL 与仅基于相似度的检索在生成正确性上的对比结果（见第 7 节）。

Conclusion: 作者的立场是：以结构性方式而非模型内部置信度来评估检索充分性，是构建私有企业环境下更安全代码生成系统的一个独立且当前被忽视的信号。

Abstract: Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) for code generation has been studied extensively on public repositories, where a model's parametric knowledge often compensates for imperfect retrieval. This breaks down in enterprise codebases, where private APIs, internal frameworks, and undocumented team conventions fall entirely outside any model's pretraining distribution. Recent work on private-library code generation shows that even oracle (perfect) retrieval does not eliminate errors, but locates failures downstream in API usage; separately, confidence-gated retrieval has been studied for open-domain question answering using model-internal confidence. Neither addresses whether retrieval itself was structurally sufficient for a private-code query before generation begins. We introduce RCL (Retrieval-Confidence Layer), a lightweight module inserted between retrieval and generation that combines a call-graph-derived structural coverage score with a novelty score estimating a query's dependence on knowledge outside the model's prior, to detect insufficient retrieval before generation occurs. When confidence falls below a calibrated threshold, RCL triggers a targeted follow-up retrieval or labels the output for human review, rather than generating silently against incomplete context. We describe RCL's architecture, formalize its scoring functions, and propose an evaluation methodology using a private-code benchmark built by injecting synthetic internal APIs into open-source Java repositories, simulating the enterprise condition without proprietary code. We report results (Section 7) comparing RCL against similarity-only retrieval on generation correctness. Our position is that retrieval sufficiency, assessed structurally rather than from model-internal confidence, is a distinct and currently underaddressed signal for building safer code-generation systems in private, enterprise settings.

</details>


### [37] [SaltBench: A Referee-Gated Protocol for Measuring Method Effects in Machine-Checked Software Work](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11076)
*Jason Hickey*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出 SaltBench 基准协议，用于测量"机器裁判"（证明内核、程序验证器或隐藏测试套件）如何改变编码智能体的工作方式，并报告了五个 Rust/Verus 系统组件的四臂实验结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器裁判单方面决定智能体成果的价值且不可争辩，但其影响难以事后叙述和量化。本文希望建立一种可测量、结果无法被事后编故事的协议，使裁判效应成为可观测对象，并预先登记预测、冻结运行条件。

Method: 设计 SaltBench 协议：所有结果在智能体工具链之外裁定；智能体与网络、参考解和测试框架隔离，并用探测探针在任何计分运行前验证隔离墙是否被攻破，使隔离是"被观测"而非"被假设"；每次运行由带日期的冻结授权并登记预测；预算停止只作中止、不计为失败。基准对象是"seat"（标准框架中的一次智能体会话），在固定 Verus 工具链下用 Rust 编写五个系统组件，以隐藏测试套件作为裁判。测试四臂：普通智能体；被要求先写规格并据此验证代码的智能体（方法的缩减呈现）；以及两臂先验提供规格、经带日期修订扩展至 k=4。

Result: 被要求"写规格并验证"的臂在全部五个组件上成本更高，且差距具有实际意义：在两种声明集解读下溢价均不超过 2.8879×，最便宜的三个低于 1.4×。符号检验未达成结论（4 中 3，p = 0.3125，所有溢价低于可分辨下限）。溢价随组件规模增大而上升，在最小组件上接近 1。

Conclusion: 该成本上界是当前这类组件总体的性质，而非对更大总体的承诺。作者发布了完整记录，以保证结果可审计、不可事后叙述。

Abstract: SaltBench is a benchmark protocol for one question: How does a machine referee change the way a coding agent works? A machine referee --- a proof kernel, a program verifier, or a withheld test suite --- decides what an agent's work is worth, and the agent cannot argue with it. Here we report a protocol that makes the referee's effect measurable and whose answers cannot be narrated afterwards: every outcome is decided outside the agent's own toolchain; the agent is walled off from the network, the reference solutions and the harness itself, and the wall is tested by probes that try to breach it before any scored run, so the isolation is observed rather than assumed; every run is authorized by a dated freeze with its predictions registered; and a budget stop is a halt, never a failure. In this study, the subject of the benchmark is a ``seat'', meaning an agent session in its standard harness. We tested five systems components, all authored in Rust under a pinned Verus toolchain, with a withheld test suite as the referee for each. Four arms are tested: a plain agent; an agent that is also instructed to create a specification and verify the code against it, in a reduced rendering of the method, as registered; and two arms where the specification is provided a priori, extended under a dated amendment to $k=4$, where the registered sign test reached no verdict (3 of 4, $p = 0.3125$, every premium below the resolvable floor). We found that the arm instructed to specify and verify cost more on all five components, and by a practical margin: across these five components no premium exceeded $2.8879\times$ under either reading of the declared set, and the three cheapest sat below $1.4\times$. That bound is a property of this population and not a promise about larger ones: the premium runs near $1$ on the smallest components and rises with size. We publish the complete record.

</details>

*Arnis Lektauers,Gusts Linkevičs,Guntis Mosāns,Arina Fokina,Rasa Gulbe*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一种以模型为中心的 DevOps 架构，将基于 DEVS 的数字孪生仿真模型作为一等制品，通过声明式 YAML 定义、CI/CD 验证与不可变版本化，部署为 Kubernetes 上的托管微服务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字孪生仿真模型像软件一样需要演进和重新部署，但基于 DEVS 的引擎虽有严谨的形式化基础，却在云原生环境下缺乏版本管理、自动化验证和持续交付支持，导致大多数部署中的模型生命周期管理处于临时、随意状态。

Method: 提出以模型为中心的 DevOps 架构：用声明式 YAML 语言定义仿真模型并形式化映射到 multiPDEVS；在 CI/CD 流水线中做结构与语义验证，生成不可变版本化制品（回滚即固定版本标识）；平台分解为 Kubernetes 上的容器化微服务，并对引擎做状态外化与生命周期控制适配。

Result: 以拉脱维亚里加 22 路公交走廊为初始案例研究（规划中的里加全市多模态交通数字孪生首个实例），完整跑通生命周期，并报告了约 47,870 个 DEVS 原子组件场景下的单容器引擎吞吐量；流水线级捕获统计与集群级多场景并发执行由配套实证研究覆盖。

Conclusion: 该架构将 DEVS 数字孪生仿真转化为可版本化、可验证、可持续交付的云原生托管服务，为解决模型生命周期管理临时化问题提供了形式化与工程化结合的方案；更大规模的流水线与集群级评估留待后续研究。

Abstract: Digital twin simulation models are evolved and redeployed like software, yet DEVS-based engines offer a sound formal basis with little support for versioning, automated validation, or continuous delivery in cloud-native environments, leaving model lifecycle management ad hoc in most deployments. This paper proposes a model-centric DevOps architecture for deploying DEVS-based digital twin simulations as managed services. Simulation models are treated as first-class DevOps artefacts defined in a declarative YAML language with a formal mapping to multiPDEVS, supporting structural and semantic validation in a CI/CD pipeline that produces immutable versioned artefacts, so that reverting to an earlier validated version reduces to pinning its identifier. The platform is decomposed into containerised microservices on Kubernetes, with engine adaptations for state externalisation and lifecycle control. An initial case study on the Riga Route 22 public-transit corridor, the first instantiation of a planned city-wide multi-modal transport digital twin for Riga, Latvia, exercises the full lifecycle and reports single-container engine throughput for a scenario with roughly 47,870 DEVS atomic components; pipeline-level catch statistics and cluster-level concurrent multi-scenario execution are the subject of companion empirical studies.

</details>


### [39] [FST Pay: Deterministic Safety-Gated Architecture for Youth Digital Payments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11195)
*Shaikh Mohammed Burhan,Syed Farhaan Quadri,Tabassum Nahid Sultana*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 FST Pay，一种面向青少年实时支付的架构与形式化规范：在实时授权路径上采用严格确定性安全门控，将生成式 AI 完全解耦至结算后下游，仅用于生成自然语言金融洞察，从而在保障金融普惠的同时规避概率性模型带来的不确定性与审计风险。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数字支付基础设施让青少年直接接入实时金融服务，早期接入虽有助于金融素养与数字普惠，但也带来冲动消费、社会工程诈骗、未授权交易和商户剥削等严重风险。传统对策依赖概率性机器学习或僵化的静态控制；而让概率性或生成式 AI 直接参与实时支付授权会引入非确定性、不可预测的边界行为与关键审计漏洞。

Method: FST Pay 基于一个不可变操作边界。对经 UPI 等通道发起的交易执行六项确定性不变式检查：消费限额、监护人共同签署策略、交易金额阈值、商户类别码、时间访问区间、硬件完整性约束。交易通过有序、互斥的决策函数被严格分类为 ALLOW、REVIEW 或 BLOCK。高风险交易触发异步监护人共同签署流程。

Result: 生成式 AI 被完全置于结算流程下游，仅消费已发布的事后决策事件来生成自然语言金融洞察，对账本不具备任何变更权限，从而实现确定性与可审计性。

Conclusion: FST Pay 通过确定性安全门控与下游 AI 解释的解耦架构，为青少年实时支付提供了一种兼顾安全性与可审计性的形式化设计方案。

Abstract: Digital payment infrastructures increasingly provide adolescent users with direct access to real-time financial services. While early access promotes financial literacy and digital inclusion, it exposes young users to severe risks of impulsive spending, social engineering frauds, unauthorized transactions, and merchant exploitation. Conventional countermeasures rely on probabilistic machine learning or rigid static controls. However, allowing probabilistic or generative artificial intelligence (AI) models to directly influence real-time payment authorization introduces non-determinism, unpredictable edge-case behavior, and critical audit vulnerabilities. This paper introduces Financial Safety for Teens Pay (FST Pay) as an architectural and formal specification. FST Pay is founded on an immutable operational boundary: strict deterministic safety gating on the real-time authorization path coupled with decoupled downstream AI explanation. Transactions initiated via rails like UPI are subjected to six deterministic invariant checks covering spending limits, guardian co-sign policies, transaction amount thresholds, merchant category codes, temporal access intervals, and hardware integrity constraints. Transactions are classified strictly into ALLOW, REVIEW, or BLOCK outcomes through an ordered, mutually exclusive decision function. High-risk transactions trigger an asynchronous guardian co-sign workflow. Generative AI is relegated entirely downstream of settlement, consuming published post-decision events solely to generate natural-language financial insights without holding mutation privileges over the ledger.

</details>


### [40] [TripleBound: Triplet-Guided Heterogeneous Graph Learning for Microservice Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11212)
*Mineth Weerasinghe,Himindu Kularathne,Methmini Madhushika,Danuka Lakshan,Nisansa de Silva,Adeesha Wijayasiri,Srinath Perera*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出 TripleBound，一种将异构图神经网络与弱监督三元组约束结合的混合框架，用于单体到微服务的自动化拆分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单体迁移到微服务面临紧耦合与服务边界不清晰的难题；现有拆分方法多只依赖结构依赖或语义相似度信号，很少在统一的表示学习目标中整合二者。

Method: 以 CHGNN 将单体建模为含程序节点、资源节点、CALL 边与 CRUD 边的异构图以捕捉结构依赖；同时基于包结构、命名规范与代码位置，由解析器推断服务组并生成弱监督三元组约束，将三元组约束直接注入共享的结构潜空间，使两类信号在表示学习中联合优化。

Result: 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader、PlantsByWebSphere 和 JPetStore 上评估：在选定权重下，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir、DayTrader 和 JPetStore 上取得最高综合拆分得分，优于 CHGNN 与 MonoEmbed，而 CHGNN 在 PlantsByWebSphere 上更强。分指标分析显示存在权衡：结构模块化与分区间接耦合的收益伴随部分数据集上实体分布不均衡加剧。更换综合权重后，TripleBound 在 AcmeAir 与 DayTrader 上仍保持第一，但在 JPetStore 上不再领先，说明总体排名依赖具体指标权重。

Conclusion: TripleBound 表明在统一表示学习中融合结构与语义信号可提升拆分效果，但优势并非在所有数据集与指标权重下都稳定，聚合排名的稳健性有待进一步研究。

Abstract: Cloud computing and DevOps have made microservices a common architecture for scalable, maintainable software systems. However, migrating monoliths to microservices remains challenging due to tight coupling and unclear service boundaries. Existing decomposition approaches typically rely on either structural dependencies or semantic similarity signals, but rarely integrate both within a unified representation learning objective. This paper proposes TripleBound, a hybrid framework for automated monolith-to-microservices decomposition that augments a heterogeneous graph neural network with weakly supervised triplet constraints derived from parser-inferred service groups based on package structure, naming conventions, and code location. TripleBound injects triplet-based constraints directly into the shared structural latent space, enabling both signals to be jointly optimized during representation learning. Structural dependencies are captured using CHGNN, which models the monolith as a heterogeneous graph with program nodes, resource nodes, CALL edges, and CRUD edges. Semantic relationships are incorporated through triplet constraints generated from parser-inferred service groups. Evaluation on AcmeAir, DayTrader, PlantsByWebSphere, and JPetStore shows that TripleBound achieves the highest composite decomposition score under the selected weighting on AcmeAir, DayTrader, and JPetStore compared to CHGNN and MonoEmbed, while CHGNN remains stronger on PlantsByWebSphere. Per-metric analysis reveals trade-offs: gains in structural modularity and inter-partition coupling are accompanied by higher entity distribution imbalance on some datasets. Alternative composite weightings preserve TripleBound's first-place ranking on AcmeAir and DayTrader but not on JPetStore, showing that the aggregate ranking is metric-dependent.

</details>

*Alessio Ferrari,Minh An Nguyen,Kushal Ramkumar,Liliana Pasquale*

TL;DR: 一项针对26名网络安全硕士生的探索性研究，通过分析ChatGPT交互日志，考察其在漏洞修复作业中的使用模式与成绩的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型正快速进入软件工程教育，但其在"安全软件工程"教育中的作用尚缺乏研究，需要理解学生如何使用LLM以及这种使用是否影响学习成效。

Method: 对某非全日制网络安全硕士项目中26名研究生在漏洞修复作业期间的ChatGPT交互日志，采用结构化双重编码流程进行编码分析，并检验使用模式与先前网络安全专业知识是否与作业成绩相关。

Result: 不同网络安全专业水平的学生采用了大致相似的ChatGPT策略；个人使用模式在成绩上存在描述性差异，但经多重比较校正后均不显著；相比之下，ChatGPT使用多样性（所采用的不同使用模式数量）与成绩呈正相关，且在控制网络安全专业知识后依然成立。

Conclusion: 学生"如何使用"ChatGPT可能比"是否使用"更具信息价值；研究结果属于探索性发现，后续需开展对照研究，以指导学生更有效地在安全软件工程教育中使用LLM。

Abstract: The rapid adoption of Large Language Models (LLMs) is reshaping software engineering education, but their role in secure software engineering education remains underexplored. We report an exploratory empirical study of how 26 graduate students in a part-time MSc Cybersecurity programme used ChatGPT during a vulnerability-fixing assignment. To characterise ChatGPT use, we analysed students' ChatGPT interaction logs using a structured double-coding procedure and examined whether usage patterns and prior cybersecurity expertise were associated with assignment performance. The results show that students with varying levels of cybersecurity expertise used broadly similar ChatGPT strategies. Individual usage patterns showed descriptive differences by grade, but none remained statistically significant after correcting for multiple comparisons. In contrast, diversity of ChatGPT usage, i.e., the number of distinct usage patterns adopted, was positively associated with performance, even after controlling for cybersecurity expertise. These exploratory findings suggest that the way students engage with ChatGPT may be more informative than whether they use it, and motivate future controlled studies to guide students toward effective LLM use in secure software engineering education.

</details>


### [42] [CoSTAR: Data Synthesis-Driven Constraint-Aware COBOL Section Summarization for Legacy System Modernization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11332)
*Hao Lin,He Jiang,Xiaochen Li,Weihong Sun,Yufu Wang,Zhilei Ren,Ang Jia*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出CoSTAR框架，通过执行验证的数据合成与约束感知训练解决COBOL段级代码摘要的数据稀缺和迁移约束保留问题，使小型本地部署LLM达到接近超大模型的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COBOL仍广泛应用于政府、金融机构和大型企业，但技术老化、人才减少和文档缺失使遗留系统现代化日益紧迫。代码摘要有助于理解遗留系统，而COBOL段级摘要面临两大挑战：数据稀缺和迁移约束保留。

Method: CoSTAR将通用编程任务改造为LLM生成的可执行验证COBOL代码摘要数据，以缓解数据稀缺；在此基础上，为目标段补充相关数据声明和自然语言解释，并使用约束引导的结构化理由训练较小的基座LLM，从而保留COBOL段摘要的迁移约束。

Result: CoSTAR有效合成了3,764条执行验证的训练实例。基于7B/8B基座LLM，ROUGE-L平均相对提升25.38%，METEOR提升53.84%，chrF提升37.22%。在真实企业评估中，仅基于Qwen3-8B的CoSTAR在准确性、完整性和简洁性上优于企业部署的Qwen3-235B。

Conclusion: CoSTAR使小型、可本地部署的LLM在隐私敏感的COBOL遗留系统中达到与超大LLM相当的性能。

Abstract: COBOL remains critical to governments, financial institutions, and large enterprises; yet, aging technologies, shrinking expertise, and missing documentation make modernization of COBOL-based legacy systems increasingly urgent. Before migration, code summarization is a common practice to support legacy system understanding. However, COBOL code summarization, especially on section-level, faces two key challenges: data scarcity and migration constraint preservation. To address these challenges, we propose CoSTAR, an integrated framework that combines execution-validated data synthesis with constraint-aware model training. CoSTAR repurposes general-purpose programming tasks to synthesize execution-validated COBOL code-summary data through LLM-based generation to overcome data scarcity. Based on the synthesized data, CoSTAR augments target sections with relevant data declarations and natural-language explanations, and uses constraint-guided structured rationales to train smaller base LLMs. The trained LLMs preserve the migration constraints for COBOL section summarization. We evaluate CoSTAR on both public and confidential enterprise COBOL systems. CoSTAR effectively synthesizes 3,764 execution-validated training instances. Based on these instances, CoSTAR built on 7B/8B base LLMs can improve these LLMs with average relative gains of 25.38% on ROUGE-L, 53.84% on METEOR, and 37.22% on chrF. In real-world enterprise evaluation, CoSTAR built on only Qwen3-8B, outperforms the enterprise-deployed Qwen3-235B in accuracy, completeness, and conciseness. These results show that CoSTAR enables small, locally deployable LLMs to achieve performance competitive with substantially larger LLMs for privacy-sensitive COBOL legacy systems.

</details>


### [43] [Agent-Integrated Software: Interaction Contracts and Continuous Assurance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11381)
*Shengcheng Yu,Chunrong Fang,Zhenyu Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出“代理集成软件(AIS)”模式与“意图级交互抽象(IIA)”，通过开放转移系统模型和交互契约，在任务层面建立委托执行与应用行为之间的显式对应，并配套持续保障机制，旨在将智能代理集成转变为一门可维护的软件工程学科。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有应用中嵌入智能代理会带来持续性的协调难题：用户可在委托执行继续进行时修改目标、操作共享对象。作者认为，要实现可靠集成，任务级交互与应用行为之间必须具备显式对应关系，而当前缺乏这种系统化的抽象与约束机制。

Method: 提出AIS软件模式（由常规核心、直接交互、内生代理组成的，并定义IIA作为用户检视与控制委托工作的任务语义，用开放转移系统模型关联AIS执行与IIA状态和事件。通过交互契约（任务绑定、角色特定权限、控制转移、结果证据）约束该关系，并以持续保障机制在依赖变化时维持限定性声明。此外，使用简洁的披露契约与条件命题说明局部组件有效性不足，以及如何将部分准入不变式与规划分离。

Result: 通过简洁的披露契约和条件命题论证了局部组件有效性是不充分的；展示了部分准入不变式可与规划相分离；并通过对比不同软件领域揭示了该框架的假设与适用边界。

Conclusion: 该研究提出了一条涵盖应用抽象、开发支持、受控执行、质量评估与人工监督的研究议程，目标是使代理集成成为一门可维护的软件工程学科。

Abstract: Embedding an intelligent agent in an existing application creates a persistent coordination problem: users can revise goals and manipulate shared objects while delegated execution continues. We argue that dependable integration requires an explicit correspondence between task-level interaction and application behavior. We introduce Agent-Integrated Software (AIS) as a software pattern combining a conventional core, direct interaction, and a built-in agent, and Intent-Level Interaction Abstraction (IIA) as the task semantics through which users inspect and control delegated work. An open transition-system model relates AIS execution to IIA states and events. Interaction contracts constrain this relation through task bindings, role-specific authority, control transitions, and outcome evidence; continuous assurance maintains scoped claims as their dependencies change. A compact disclosure contract and conditional propositions illustrate why local component validity is insufficient and how selected admission invariants can be separated from planning. Contrasting software domains expose the framework's assumptions and limits. This perspective develops a research agenda spanning application abstraction, development support, controlled execution, quality assessment, and human supervision, with the aim of making agent integration a maintainable software engineering discipline.

</details>

Abstract: Effective bug triage is crucial for streamlining the software development lifecycle by accurately categorizing and assigning reported software defects. In this paper, we propose an automated bug triage system built upon the pre-trained RoBERTa-base transformer architecture. By leveraging deep contextual representations, our approach efficiently classifies incoming bug reports to optimize assignment. Experimental evaluation demonstrates that the proposed system achieves a strong bug identification accuracy of 0.90 within just five training epochs. These findings highlight the efficiency and high performance of fine-tuned transformer models for practical software engineering automation.

</details>


### [45] [ChurnBench: A Drift-Aware Benchmark Demonstrating That Refresh Scheduling, Not Cache Age, Governs Staleness in Agentic AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11515)
*Vivek Kumar Singh,Preeti Priyam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 ChurnBench，一个以时间线而非快照方式生成企业数据织物的开源基准，通过追加式真值账本区分"新鲜度错误"与"推理错误"，并发现缓存年龄不预测过期，调度刷新才是关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有检索基准将数据冻结为快照，只能评估智能体是否找到正确段落，无法判断其答案在当前时刻是否仍然正确。生产环境中的许可证再分配、用户离职、价格变动、合同续签等持续变化，使得"答案曾经正确但现已失效"的问题无法被现有基准捕捉。

Method: 构建 ChurnBench：生成一个四源企业数据织物，采用时间线形式而非快照；所有变更写入仅追加的真值账本；金标准答案从账本计算而非来自实时存储。对每个样本在两个时间戳上解析真值，若答案在数据被检索时正确但评估时错误，则标记为新鲜度错误，与推理错误区分。通过缓存年龄为 1、14、28 天的设置，以及禁用分层刷新的受控消融实验来验证机制。

Result: 在缓存年龄 1、14、28 天下，新鲜度错误分别为 7、4、4，说明调度刷新通过 TTL 限制了过期程度，且未观察到任何 TTL 失效窗口。受控消融显示：禁用分层刷新后，28 天下的新鲜度错误从 4 升至 45，而 1 天下保持不变。

Conclusion: 缓存年龄并不能预测答案过期；真正应扫描的变量是 TTL 配置与各实体变化率之间的匹配关系，而非漂移窗口长度。ChurnBench、评测工具及全部逐错误数据均开源发布。

Abstract: In production, agentic systems answer questions over data that lives in several places and keeps changing: licenses are reassigned, users offboarded, prices changed, contracts renewed. Existing retrieval benchmarks freeze the data, so they cannot ask whether an agent's answer is still true, only whether it found the right passage. We present ChurnBench, an open-source benchmark that generates a four-source enterprise data fabric as a timeline rather than a snapshot. Every change is written to an append-only ground-truth ledger, and gold answers are computed from that ledger, never from the live stores. An answer that was correct when its data was retrieved but wrong when evaluated is therefore detected and labeled a freshness error, distinct from a reasoning error; we validate this by resolving ground truth at both timestamps for every case reported. Using the instrument, we find that when a system refreshes on a schedule, cache age does not predict staleness. Across cache ages of 1, 14, and 28 days, freshness errors were 7, 4, and 4, because scheduled refresh bounds staleness by time-to-live, and no TTL lapse was observed in any window. A controlled ablation confirms the mechanism: disabling tiered refresh raises freshness errors from 4 to 45 at 28 days and leaves them identical at one day. The variable a drift benchmark should sweep is therefore TTL configuration against each entity's rate of change, not drift-window length. ChurnBench, the evaluation harness, and all per-error data are released open source.

</details>


### [46] [PRISMA-LLM: An Empirical Reporting Framework for AI-Assisted Systematic Reviews](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11559)
*Miguel Zabaleta,Baihan Lin*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文分析了一个包含888篇综述自动化论文、14,726条标注的语料库SciLitBench，刻画了方法、综述阶段使用、评估与局限报告的变化，并据此提出PRISMA-LLM框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM与AI软件越来越多地参与系统综述决策，但审计这些工作流所需的信息报告不一致，缺乏可审计的规范。

Method: 构建并分析SciLitBench语料库（888篇论文、14,726条标注），统计自动化在方法、综述阶段、评估方式及局限报告上的变化趋势。

Result: 自动化转向面向LLM与软件的流程，包括可改变证据基础的阶段；自2023年起，软件/产品类论文38.0%未报告评估，LLM类为9.3%；报告覆盖随LLM工作流复杂度上升，但52%仅报告正面结果的LLM评估仍存在未满足的可靠性或性能要求。

Conclusion: 基于这些模式，提出PRISMA-LLM框架，区分"实现披露"与"后果敏感评估及局限报告"，以实现对LLM辅助系统综述的审计。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) and AI-enabled software increasingly participate in systematic-review decisions, yet the information needed to audit these workflows is reported inconsistently. We analyze SciLitBench, a corpus of 888 review-automation papers with 14,726 annotations, to characterize changes in methods, review-stage use, evaluation and reported limitations. Automation has shifted toward LLM- and software-facing workflows, including stages that can alter the evidence base. Since 2023, 38.0% of software/product papers reported no evaluation, compared with 9.3% of LLM papers. Reporting coverage increased with LLM workflow complexity, yet 52% of positive-only LLM evaluations still reported an unmet reliability or performance requirement. From these patterns, we introduce PRISMA-LLM, an empirically grounded framework separating implementation disclosure from consequence-sensitive evaluation and limitation reporting.

</details>


### [47] [Ecdysis: Efficient and Effective Training of Runtime Harnesses for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11677)
*Ruiqing Yue,Yu Cui,Zhuoyu Sun,Sicheng Pan,Xianhong Xue,Tingyu Li,Ting Li,Wenzhuo Zhu,Yi Chen,Yifei Liu,Baohan Huang,Zhe Cui,Haibin Zhang,Cong Zuo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出 Ecdysis 框架，通过跨任务失败模式诊断，区分模型特定适配与框架级修复，实现更高效、泛化更好的 LLM 智能体框架进化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架进化方法依赖迭代搜索，反复执行与修改代码，时间开销大，且易过拟合已观测任务与特定失败模式，泛化能力差。作者指出缺乏有原则的失败诊断是关键瓶颈：单个失败可能源于模型特定缺陷或系统性框架缺陷，直接针对个体失败优化会导致不必要的模型特定适配。

Method: Ecdysis 区分模型特定适配与框架级修复，偏向修复系统性的框架缺陷。采用批级跨实例失败聚合范式，联合分析多个任务实例的失败证据；引入"失败驱动的协同精化"（Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement），通过多角色诊断来判明失败原因，并迭代精化框架修改规范。

Result: 相比现有框架进化方法，Ecdysis 在框架训练上实现最高 1.84 倍加速，同时使所得框架的推理准确率提升 18.56%。

Conclusion: 将跨实例失败分析与多角色诊断相结合，可在更低训练时间下实现更有效的框架进化，兼顾效率与泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving runtime harnesses can substantially improve the capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents and provide a promising paradigm for optimizing agent execution. Existing harness evolution methods typically rely on iterative search, repeatedly evaluating and revising candidate harnesses based on execution feedback from task instances. While this paradigm enables continuous harness optimization, it incurs substantial time overhead due to repeated agent executions and code modifications, and may overfit to observed tasks and specific failure patterns, resulting in degraded generalization to unseen tasks. We identify the lack of principled failure diagnosis as a key bottleneck in harness evolution: an observed failure can reflect either model-specific deficiencies or systematic harness deficiencies, and directly optimizing against individual failures can lead to unnecessary model-specific accommodation. We therefore propose Ecdysis, an efficient and effective framework that distinguishes model-specific accommodation from harness-level repair and biases adaptation toward systematic harness deficiencies by identifying recurring cross-task failure patterns. Ecdysis adopts a batch-level cross-instance failure aggregation paradigm to jointly analyze failure evidence from multiple task instances and further introduces Failure-Driven Collaborative Refinement to diagnose failure causes and iteratively refine harness modification specifications. By combining cross-instance failure analysis with multi-role diagnosis, Ecdysis enables more effective harness evolution with lower training time. Experiments show that Ecdysis achieves up to a 1.84x speedup in harness training compared with existing harness evolution methods, while improving the reasoning accuracy of the resulting harnesses by 18.56%.

</details>


### [48] [Reproducibility in the Age of Agentic AI: Context Engineering at the Timescale of a Codebase](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11728)
*Lorena A. Barba*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该文提出可将"可复现研究实践"视为面向AI编码代理的上下文工程：这些实践为代理提供可用的上下文，代理则降低其维护成本并让收益即时显现，但研究者仍需对相关产物及其编码的科学判断负责。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动机：AI编码代理已广泛进入科研与开发流程，但如何让它们在研究中产出可靠、可复现的结果仍缺乏框架性认识。作者希望把可复现性实践与代理的上下文需求对接，说明二者互为支撑，并澄清人类研究者的责任边界。

Method: 方法：本文属论证性/立场性（position）文章，通过概念分析展开论证：将测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令文档与决策记录等可复现性产物重新定义为代理的"上下文"，并分析代理在维护这些上下文时的成本—收益结构，进而推导研究者的责任归属。

Result: 结果：提出核心论点——可复现研究实践本质上是针对AI编码代理的上下文工程。代理显著降低了维护测试、提交历史、仓库结构、指令与决策记录的成本，同时使其收益（如更好的可追踪性、可审计性与协作效率）变得更加直接和即时。

Conclusion: 结论：即便代理承担了大量维护工作，验证这些产物及其所编码的科学判断的责任仍在于研究者本人。可复现性实践不是可外包的技术细节，而是研究者与代理协作的科学责任所在。

Abstract: Reproducible research practices are context engineering for AI coding agents. I argue that agents lower the cost of maintaining tests, commit histories, repository structure, instructions, and decision records while making their benefits immediate. Researchers remain responsible for verifying these artifacts and the scientific judgments they encode.

</details>


### [49] [An analysis of the relationship of input metrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11824)
*Addison Crump*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了划分测试中的输入度量指标，指出常规经验比较方法的不足，并提出改进的$k$-alt-path度量，同时为未来输入度量研究建立了分析策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 输入度量通过测试套件中输入的覆盖特征来评估测试进展。自20世纪50年代起已有多种此类度量被提出，但少有研究对其进行比较。本文旨在填补这一空白，借助划分测试文献中用于其他度量类别的方法来比较输入度量。

Method: 首先定义并回顾常见输入度量；通过一个简短案例研究揭示典型经验比较策略在度量比较上存在根本性不足；随后提出并实现$k$-alt-path——一种新度量，在$k$-path基础上减少冗余并提升敏感性；最后系统比较其他常见输入度量并讨论其启示。

Result: 案例研究证明典型经验比较策略不足以比较度量；所提出的$k$-alt-path相比$k$-path减少了冗余并提高了敏感性；对其他常见输入度量进行了系统比较并得出相应发现。

Conclusion: 本文提出了划分测试分析方法，为输入度量的改进提供了合理依据，并形成了未来相关研究的策略方向。

Abstract: Input metrics evaluate the progress of testing in terms of features of inputs present in a test suite. Previous works, as early as the 1950s, established a number of such metrics, but few endeavored to compare them. This paper does so by utilizing existing methods proposed for other metric classes in partition testing literature. After defining and reviewing common input metrics, we begin with a short case study revealing that typical empirical comparison strategies are fundamentally insufficient for comparing metrics. Then, we demonstrate how one rigorously improves a standard metric by defining and implementing $k$-alt-path, a new metric which reduces redundancy while improving sensitivity over $k$-path. Each of the other common input metrics are then systematically compared before discussing the implications of our findings. With these contributions, we bring forward partition testing analysis methods that justify and form a strategy for future research in refining input metrics.

</details>

### [50] [Multi-Faceted Evaluation and Mitigation of Emotion Hallucinations in MLLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11154)
*Bowen Zeng,Peipei Song,Weidong Chen,Shengeng Tang,Song Ye,Yuanhong Zhong,Beier Zhu,Xun Yang*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出EHR评估器量化多模态大模型在六个情感理解维度上的幻觉，并提出免训练的HMER框架进行细粒度幻觉缓解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型在开放式情感理解中存在情感幻觉，而现有评估因情感理解的多元认知维度和自由形式语言表达而不足，且现有缓解方法往往顾此失彼。

Method: 提出EHR（情感幻觉率）评估器，覆盖表达、动作、音频、本能、逻辑、结论六个维度；并基于发现提出HMER免训练框架，包含记录局部幻觉主张的幻觉记忆（用于定向logit校正）和保存可靠中间推理状态的锚点记忆（用于稳定后续生成）。

Result: EHR揭示现有缓解方法在某些维度降低幻觉的同时加剧其他维度；在19个MLLM上的实验证明了情感幻觉的普遍性及HMER在不同模型架构上的有效性。

Conclusion: 情感幻觉的评估需细粒度、多维度的定位与缓解，HMER通过选择性抑制不可靠线索、保留可信推理上下文，实现了跨维度的细粒度幻觉缓解。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (MLLMs) have shown strong potential in open-ended emotion understanding, yet they often generate emotion hallucinations. Evaluating such hallucinations is particularly challenging for two reasons. First, emotion understanding spans multiple cognitive facets, from multimodal perception to psychological reasoning. Second, emotional interpretations are expressed in free-form language, making existing closed-ended protocols insufficient for evaluation. To address these challenges, we introduce EHR (Emotion Hallucination Rate), an evaluator that quantifies emotion hallucinations across six facets: expression, action, audio, instinct, logic, and conclusion. Using EHR, we reveal that existing mitigation methods often reduce hallucinations in some facets while aggravating them in others, exposing the limitation of coarse-grained correction and the need for facet-aware localization and mitigation. Motivated by this finding, we propose HMER (Hallucination-aware Memory-guided Emotion Reasoning), a training-free framework for emotion hallucination mitigation. HMER maintains a Hallucination Memory that records localized hallucinated claims and enables targeted logit rectification, together with an Anchor Memory that preserves reliable intermediate reasoning states to stabilize subsequent generation. By selectively suppressing unreliable cues while preserving trustworthy reasoning context, HMER enables fine-grained mitigation across diverse hallucination facets. Extensive experiments on 19 MLLMs demonstrate the prevalence of emotion hallucinations and the effectiveness of our framework across diverse model architectures.

</details>


### [51] [Multimodal Temporal Modeling for Continuous Group Emotion Recognition in Multi-party Dialogues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11164)
*Soma Iwata,Koji Inoue,Muyun Wu,Taiga Mori,Divesh Lala,Tatsuya Kawahara*

Main category: cs.MM

TL;DR: 本文提出以一秒分辨率连续识别多方对话中的群体情绪（效价与唤醒度），引入刻画参与者情绪分歧的"混合状态（Mixed state）"，基于TEIDAN语料构建帧级软标签数据集，并提出融合音视频的滑动窗口多模态时序框架，实验证明时序Transformer优于基线与LLM模型，且高混合值区间识别误差更大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为实现多方对话场景下对话智能体的自然行为，需整体理解群体情绪（效价、唤醒度）。既有工作多停留在话语级或粗粒度时间窗，难以捕捉情绪动态变化，且缺乏对群体内情绪分歧的刻画。

Method: 将群体情绪识别建模为一秒分辨率的连续识别任务，并引入"混合状态"来刻画群体参与者间的情绪分歧；基于TEIDAN语料构建帧级软标签数据集；提出使用滑动窗口上下文整合音频与视频信息的多模态时序框架，核心为时序Transformer。

Result: 时序Transformer优于简单基线，且与真值标签的时序一致性优于基于LLM的模型；上下文长度影响有限，而音视频输入在连续标签指标上优于任一单模态输入；高混合值区间的群体情绪识别误差更大。

Conclusion: 连续、细粒度的群体情绪识别具有可行性，多模态融合优于单模态；情绪分歧（高混合值）是群体情绪识别的关键挑战，值得后续深入研究。

Abstract: To realize natural behavior in dialogue agents in multi-party dialogue scenarios, it is important to understand group emotion such as valence and arousal as a whole. Most prior work addressed this task at the utterance level or using a coarse-grained time window, which is not sufficient to capture emotional dynamics. In this study, we formulate continuous recognition of the Group Emotion at a one-second resolution. Moreover, we also introduce the Mixed state, which captures the emotional divergence among participants in the group. We constructed a dataset with frame-level soft labels based on the TEIDAN corpus and propose a multimodal temporal framework that integrates audio and video information using a sliding-window context. Experimental results demonstrate that the temporal Transformer outperforms simple baselines and shows stronger temporal agreement with the ground-truth labels than the LLM-based model. The effect of context length is limited, whereas audio-visual input outperforms either unimodal input on the continuous-label metrics. Additionally, our analysis shows larger Group Emotion recognition errors in intervals with high Mixed values, exposing emotional divergence as a key challenge for group emotion recognition.

</details>

### [52] [M3-Former: Multimodal Transformer with Mixture-of-Experts for Long-Term Vessel Trajectory Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10559)
*Wenzhe Jin,Haina Tang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 M3-Former，一个由大语言模型增强的多模态船舶轨迹预测框架，通过语义先验、双粒度 MoE 和转向加权损失，在丹麦 AIS 数据集上实现 1-4 小时预测的领先性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对船舶轨迹预测中的三大挑战：行为多模态性、语义利用率有限以及长期误差累积，作者希望引入语义先验来增强长期建模能力。

Method: 构建统一多模态表示空间，用预训练 LLM 编码船舶静态属性与航行意图等静态语义信息，并通过自注意力与动态轨迹特征对齐。引入双粒度 Mixture-of-Experts（MoE）：序列级专家建模全局航线规划，token 级专家细化局部机动行为。另设计转向加权交叉熵损失（Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy）以缓解稀疏转向样本的长尾分布问题。

Result: 在真实丹麦 AIS 数据集上，M3-Former 在 1 至 4 小时各预测时域均优于现有最先进基线；在 4 小时预测中，ADE 与 FDE 分别降低 4.4% 和 5.1%。定性分析与消融实验表明语义融合有效减少长期轨迹漂移，双粒度 MoE 提升复杂航道与航路分支场景的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该框架建立了语义引导的分层预测范式，将高层航行意图与局部运动动态联合建模，实现稳健的长期船舶轨迹预测。

Abstract: To address the challenges of behavioral multimodality, limited semantic utilization, and long-term error accumulation in vessel trajectory prediction, this paper proposes M3-Former, a multimodal trajectory prediction framework enhanced by large language models (LLMs). The proposed framework incorporates vessel static attributes and navigational intent as semantic priors for long-term trajectory modeling. Specifically, a unified multimodal representation space is constructed, in which static semantic information is encoded by a pre-trained LLM and aligned with dynamic trajectory features through self-attention. To jointly capture global route planning and local motion variations, a dual-granularity Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architecture is introduced, where sequence-level experts model global navigation trends and token-level experts refine fine-grained maneuvering behaviors. In addition, a Steering-Weighted Cross-Entropy loss is designed to alleviate the long-tail distribution of sparse turning samples and improve prediction accuracy in critical maneuvering scenarios. Experiments on a real-world Danish AIS dataset demonstrate that M\textsuperscript{3}-Former consistently outperforms state-of-the-art baselines across prediction horizons from 1 to 4 hours. In the 4-hour prediction task, the proposed method reduces Average Displacement Error (ADE) and Final Displacement Error (FDE) by 4.4\% and 5.1\%, respectively, compared with the strongest baseline. Qualitative and ablation analyses further verify that semantic fusion effectively reduces long-term trajectory drift, while the dual-granularity MoE improves robustness in complex waterways and route-branching scenarios. The proposed framework establishes a semantic-guided hierarchical prediction paradigm, in which high-level navigational intent and local motion dynamics are jointly modeled for robust long-term vessel trajectory forecasting.

</details>


### [53] [Halo: Improving forecast accuracy through heteroscedastic estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10589)
*Adam Cataldo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Halo 通过让深度预测器额外输出尺度参数并以负对数似然训练，不仅改善不确定性量化，还提升点估计精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异方差预测通常用于不确定性量化，但在时间序列之外有负面结果报告；本文旨在验证异方差估计是否能提升点估计本身。

Method: 提出 Halo，复用现有深度预测器架构，增加一个尺度输出，并在匹配的高斯/拉普拉斯负对数似然下训练；在 transformer、图网络+VAE、单层卷积网络上验证。

Result: 在标准基准的五个电价市场上，30 个模型-市场-指标对比中 28 个改善 MSE 和 MAE，平均 MSE 降低 2.6%–16.5%，平均 MAE 降低 1.7%–11.0%。

Conclusion: 是否估计尺度比尺度来源（第二个投影头还是完整并行网络）更重要；在已为点估计基线调好的超参数下仍有效，重新调参可选。

Abstract: Heteroscedastic forecasting, where a network estimates a scale parameter alongside a location parameter, is normally motivated by uncertainty quantification. This paper shows it also improves the point estimate, in contrast to reported negative results for heteroscedastic estimation outside time series. Halo is a modification that reuses an existing deep forecaster's architecture, giving it a second output for the scale of its implied distribution and training it under the matching negative log likelihood. Adapting three state-of-the-art models --- a transformer, a graph network paired with a variational autoencoder, and a single-layer convolutional network --- under both Gaussian and Laplacian losses demonstrates the phenomenon. On the five electricity price markets of a standard forecasting benchmark, Halo improves MSE and MAE in 28 of 30 model-market-metric comparisons, cutting average MSE by 2.6% to 16.5% and average MAE by 1.7% to 11.0%. Two findings emerge: (1) whether the scale estimate comes from a second projection head or from a full parallel network matters far less than whether the network estimates scale, and (2) the improvement holds under the hyperparameters already tuned for the point-estimate baseline, so retuning is optional.

</details>


### [54] [Zero-shot rib design: merging training-free generative prior with topology optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10643)
*Yongmin Kwon,Namwoo Kang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该工作将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）将其融入密度基拓扑优化的物理循环中，让文本提示成为可机器解释的工程意图表示，从而引导生成高刚度/低柔度结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然承力模式（叶脉、骨小梁、蛛网）以极高质量比实现高刚度，但经典拓扑优化器难以复现这类几何，且很少有方法能让工程师用自然语言表达结构设计意图。作者希望借助文本到图像扩散模型的生成先验作为免训练的、可复用的设计知识，把语言提示转化为可解释的工程意图，并让物理决定哪些生成特征被保留。

Method: 提出将冻结的文本到图像扩散模型作为免训练设计知识源，通过分数蒸馏采样（SDS）把提示诱导的生成梯度与有限元灵敏度在每个优化迭代中结合，在密度基拓扑优化的物理循环内蒸馏生成先验；文本提示成为显式、机器可解释的意图表示，由物理决定提示诱导特征的存留。引入带β延续的Heaviside投影来解决扩散—物理耦合中的中间密度倾向，并搭建基于骨架的自动化流程将优化密度场转为面向CAD的候选几何。跨领域迁移仅需更换文本提示，各新问题物理设置单独指定。

Result: 在覆盖4个几何域、2类物理机制的245次主要SDS运行中，49个提示—域组合里有38个实现统计显著柔度下降（力学最高-31.5%，热弹性最高-23.0%），优于基于梯度的基线。跨域形态分析发现反复出现的改进结构特征：多数域中生成先验抑制肋骨架中的死端分支，端点—柔度相关性r=+0.56至+0.99。带β延续的Heaviside投影将中间密度比例从42.6%降至<3%。基于骨架的自动化流程可将优化密度场转为可面向CAD的候选几何。

Conclusion: 通过在文本提示层面跨域、跨载荷、跨物理目标重定向生成先验（各新问题的物理设置单独指定），该框架把预训练生成模型用作工程设计的可复用、免训练先验，实现语言可表达的设计意图并达到优于基线的刚度性能。

Abstract: Natural load-bearing patterns such as leaf venation, trabecular bone, and spider webs achieve high stiffness per unit mass, yet classical topology optimizers rarely reach such geometries, and few let engineers express structural design intent through natural language. This work treats a frozen text-to-image diffusion model as a training-free source of design knowledge and distills it into the physics loop of density-based topology optimization via score distillation sampling, so that a text prompt becomes an explicit, machine-interpretable representation of engineer intent. The prompt-induced generative gradient and the finite element sensitivity are combined at every iteration, letting physics decide which prompt-induced features survive. In 245 primary SDS runs spanning four geometric domains and two physics regimes, 38 of 49 prompt--domain combinations achieved statistically significant compliance reductions (up to $-31.5\%$ mechanical and $-23.0\%$ thermoelastic), outperforming gradient-based baselines. Cross-domain morphological analysis identifies a recurring structural signature of improvement: in most domains the generative prior suppresses dead-end branches in the rib skeleton, with endpoint--compliance correlation $r = +0.56$ to $+0.99$. A Heaviside projection with $β$-continuation resolves a pronounced intermediate-density tendency in this diffusion--physics coupling ($42.6\%$ to $<3\%$), and an automated skeleton-based pipeline converts optimized density fields into \rev{candidate geometry ready for computer-aided design. By retargeting the generative prior across domains, loading conditions, and physics objectives through a change of text prompt, with each new problem's physics setup specified separately, the framework uses a pretrained generative model as a reusable, training-free prior for engineering design.

</details>


### [55] [Byzantine-Robust Federated Fire Detection with a Rotating Coordinator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10647)
*Georgia Argyrou,Aymen Bahrouny,Hedi Fendriy,Alexander Jung*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究联邦学习在室内火灾检测中的应用，提出数据集、压缩边缘检测器与半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒聚合方法，兼顾带宽、恶意客户端与固定服务器信任三大问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 室内火灾检测系统使用边缘摄像头，其敏感视频难以集中收集；现有联邦方案存在三个实际障碍：上行带宽有限、拜占庭（恶意或故障）客户端、以及对单一永久固定聚合服务器的无条件信任。

Method: （i）整合八个公开来源构建精选室内火灾检测数据集；（ii）设计可在边缘部署的检测器，模型更新压缩最高10倍；（iii）提出半去中心化拜占庭鲁棒联邦学习方法，结合历史感知聚合与轮换协调者。

Result: 压缩最高10倍仅带来平衡准确率的少量损失；轮换协调者方法能清除逐轮过滤遗漏的隐蔽攻击，消除固定服务器单点故障；在留出测试集上其准确率与检测速度与固定服务器版本相当；六节点物理分布式云部署验证了可行性。

Conclusion: 该工作同时解决带宽、拜占庭客户端与固定服务器信任三大障碍，证明轮换协调者的半去中心化联邦方法在室内火灾检测中可行且性能不逊于固定服务器方案。

Abstract: We study the application of federated learning (FL) to indoor fire detection. Such fire-detection systems use edge cameras that record sensitive footage which cannot easily be collected at a central server. Existing federated solutions leave three practical obstacles unaddressed: limited uplink bandwidth, Byzantine (malicious or faulty) clients, and unconditional trust in a single, permanently fixed aggregation server. Our main contributions address all three. In particular, we provide (i) a curated indoor fire-detection dataset assembled from eight public sources; (ii) an edge-deployable detector whose model updates are compressed up to 10 time with only a small loss in balanced accuracy; and (iii) a semi-decentralized Byzantine-robust FL method that combines history-aware aggregation with a rotating coordinator, evicting stealthy attacks that per-round filters miss while removing the fixed-server single point of failure. On the held-out test set the rotating-coordinator method matches its fixed-server counterpart in accuracy and detection speed, and a physically distributed six-node cloud deployment confirms feasibility.

</details>


### [56] [Artificial Intelligence Algorithms for the Detection of Pathologies Related to Lung Cancer through Image Analysis using Convolutional Neural Networks and Data Augmentation: a systematic mapping of the literature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10652)
*Pablo Ramirez Amador*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文综述了2015年以来人工智能与深度学习在肺癌放射影像检测中的应用，重点为卷积神经网络、迁移学习与数据增强，指出其具备高敏感性与特异性，但仍面临数据标准化、模型可解释性、隐私及伦理等挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 肺癌是全球主要致死原因之一，早期诊断对改善患者预后和生活质量至关重要。然而医学影像解读过程复杂，需依赖受过培训的专家，因此希望借助AI与深度学习实现影像分析的自动化与优化。

Method: 在PubMed、IEEEXPLORE、Scopus和Web of Science等科学数据库中进行详尽检索，筛选出96篇2015年至今关于AI与深度学习在生物医学工程中应用的文献，并进行综述分析。

Result: 结果显示，AI与深度学习可为肺癌早期诊断提供有效替代方案，具有较高的敏感性和特异性；其中卷积神经网络结合迁移学习与数据增强被视为提升影像解读准确性与效率的重要技术。

Conclusion: AI与深度学习对肺癌患者诊疗具有积极影响，但需进一步研究与监管，以应对数据缺乏标准化、模型可解释性、患者隐私以及伦理和社会影响等限制与挑战，从而确保其质量、可靠性与临床安全应用。

Abstract: Lung cancer is one of the leading causes of death worldwide, and its early diagnosis is crucial to improving patients prognosis and quality of life. However, the process of interpreting medical images for the detection of lung cancer is complex and requires trained experts. In this context, artificial intelligence (AI) and deep learning (DL) emerge as potential tools to automate and optimize image analysis. The objective of this work is to review the most recent and relevant applications of AI and DL in the field of radiology for the detection of lung cancer. To this end, an exhaustive search was carried out in scientific databases such as PubMed,IEEEXPLORE, Scopus and Web of Science, and 96 articles published from 2015 to the present addressing the use of AI and DL in biomedical engineering were selected. Emphasis is placed on the use of convolutional neural networks (CNN) with transfer learning and Data Augmentation as promising techniques to improve the accuracy and efficiency of the image interpretation process. The results show that the use of AI and DL can offer an effective alternative for the early diagnosis of lung cancer, with high sensitivity and specificity. However, current limitations and challenges that must be addressed to guarantee its responsible and safe application in clinical practice are also identified, such as the lack of standardized data, the ex plainability of the models, patient privacy, and the ethical and social implications. It is concluded that the use of AI and DL can have a positive impact on the care of patients with lung cancer, but further research and regulation are required to ensure its quality and reliability.

</details>


### [57] [GEOSTEER: Geodesic Optimization for Activation Steering in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10658)
*Xuan Cuong Ngo,Hao Vo,Ngan Le*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 GeoSteer，一种基于优化的保范激活引导方法，将引导建模为黎曼优化问题，通过表示流形上的一系列小测地线步更新激活，在多个基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 激活引导可在推理时修改 LLM 隐藏激活以控制模型行为。其中保范引导旨在不改变激活范数的情况下改变行为，降低表示崩溃与退化风险。但现有保范方法受限于预定义的引导轨迹和一步更新，难以捕捉激活分布的复杂结构。

Method: GeoSteer 将引导形式化为黎曼优化问题，在表示流形上通过一系列小测地线步迭代更新激活；并学习一个非线性激活空间目标函数以区分期望与不期望的激活，用其自适应地指导每一步引导，避免固定引导方向。

Result: 在 TruthfulQA、RealToxicityPrompts 和 UltraFeedback 基准上，GeoSteer 一致优于最先进的激活引导基线方法，引导行为更平滑、更稳定、更一致，同时保持激活范数。

Conclusion: 用自适应的、几何感知的优化替代预定义的一步编辑，可使保范激活引导更有效。

Abstract: Activation steering provides a lightweight way to control large language models (LLMs) by modifying their hidden activations at inference time. Among these approaches, norm-preserving steering aims to change model behavior without altering the activation norm, reducing the risk of representation collapse and degradation. However, existing norm-preserving methods are limited by predefined steering trajectories and by their reliance on one-step updates, which may fail to capture the complex structure of activation distributions. We propose GeoSteer, an optimization-based method for norm-preserving activation steering. GeoSteer formulates steering as a Riemannian optimization problem and updates activations through a sequence of small geodesic steps on the representation manifold. To avoid fixed steering directions, GeoSteer learns a nonlinear activation-space objective that distinguishes desired from undesired activations, and uses this function to adaptively guide each steering step. This multistep formulation yields smoother, more stable, and more consistent steering behavior while preserving the activation norm. Across TruthfulQA, RealToxicityPrompts, and UltraFeedback benchmarks, GeoSteer consistently improves over state-of-the-art activation steering baselines. These results suggest that norm-preserving steering can be made more effective by replacing predefined one-step edits with adaptive, geometry-aware optimization.

</details>


### [58] [Conformal Calibration Transfer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10737)
*Achref Doula*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC)，在仅有源空间标注校准数据、目标空间无标签但有配对观测的情形下，将源校准传输到目标空间并校正残余失配，实现目标域覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准共形预测依赖校准数据与部署数据间的可交换性假设，但当标注校准仅存在于源空间、而预测集需用于通过无标签配对观测（如配对模态或传感器变化）与源相连的目标空间时，该假设不再成立，因此需要研究共形校准迁移问题。

Method: 提出TCC方法：先利用配对数据将带标签的源校准传输到目标空间，再仅用无标签目标输入校正传输后的残余失配。具体给出两种互补校正方法：TCC-KS使用无标签不确定性代理检测失配并保守调整校准；weighted-TCC在权重稳定时对传输校准进行重加权以提升效率。理论上提供随可观测失配度量自适应的有限样本目标域覆盖保证。

Result: 在CIFAR-100-C、Tiny-ImageNet-C和SEN12MS上验证，无需目标域标注校准数据即可实现可靠的目标域覆盖迁移，且无标签诊断指标可预测何时需要校正。

Conclusion: TCC在缺乏目标域标注校准的情况下有效解决了共形校准迁移问题，兼具覆盖可靠性与效率，并通过无标签诊断提供实用性指导。

Abstract: Conformal prediction converts point predictions into set-valued predictions with coverage guarantees under exchangeability between calibration and deployment data. We study conformal calibration transfer, where this requirement fails because labeled calibration is available only in a source space, while prediction sets are needed in a target space linked to the source through unlabeled paired observations (e.g., paired modalities or sensor changes). We propose Transported Conformal Calibration (TCC): we transport labeled source calibration into the target space using the paired data, and then correct residual post-transport mismatch using only unlabeled target inputs. We instantiate this correction with two complementary methods: TCC-KS, which uses a label-free uncertainty surrogate to detect mismatch and adjust calibration conservatively, and weighted-TCC, which reweights transported calibration toward the target domain for improved efficiency when weights are stable. We provide finite-sample target-domain coverage guarantees that adapt to an observable measure of mismatch. Across CIFAR-100-C, Tiny-ImageNet-C, and SEN12MS, we show reliable target-domain coverage transfer without labeled target calibration data, with label-free diagnostics that predict when correction is needed.

</details>


### [59] [The Truth Was Never Gone: Perfect Aliasing in Compliant-Context Truth Probes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10739)
*Dylan Jayabahu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在"truth probe"中，当真实报告与任务规定动作在合规语境下重合时，仅凭拟合标签无法区分二者，作者称之为"完美混叠"，并通过二元报告博弈、随机码本与混合拟合实验加以验证与拆解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作常用线性探针来检测模型的"真实信念"或"欺骗"，但当真实报告与任务规定动作在训练语境中恰好一致时，探针拟合的标签无法区分其测量目标究竟是"真相"还是"规定动作"，这构成语义识别的失败，需要被明确刻画并加以实验验证。

Method: 构建受控的二元报告博弈：在合规语境（truth 与 prescribed action 一致）与对手语境（二者互补）下拟合 truth probe 与 prescribed-action probe。用随机码本把规定输出符号与语义动作分离，再用合规与对手语境混合拟合将 truth 与规定动作分离。在奖励训练过的 Gemma-2-9B 策略上，跨三个训练种子比较常规探针与混合拟合探针在留出对手激活上的 AUROC。

Result: 在对手语境中，两类探针标签互为补集，其 AUROC 之和恒为 1，该恒等式在 751 个"单元-层"对上以浮点精度成立。对在所有评估对手试次中都给出虚假回答的 Gemma-2-9B 策略，常规探针 AUROC 仅为 0.006±0.005（三个种子），而混合拟合探针在同一留出激活上达到 1.000。此外，两个在分布内都完美的合规拟合探针，在同一对手激活上分别得 0.080 与 0.986。

Conclusion: 研究结论限于"探针测量的是什么"这一层面：完美混叠揭示了仅凭拟合标签无法完成语义识别。结果说明线性可恢复性，并未证明模型保留了功能性信念、被恢复方向具有因果作用，或存在可部署的欺骗检测器。混合拟合使用更多样本且接触带标注的对手语境，因此该比较不能单独归因于去相关的收益。

Abstract: A truth probe fitted where truthful reporting and a task's prescribed action coincide cannot distinguish those targets from its fitting labels alone. We call this failure of semantic identification perfect aliasing. In a controlled binary reporting game, truth and prescribed-action probes fitted on compliant contexts solve the same optimization. On rival contexts their labels are complements, forcing their AUROCs to sum to one; this identity holds across 751 cell-layer pairs to floating-point precision. We separate prescribed output symbols from semantic action using randomized codebooks, then separate truth from prescribed action by fitting on mixed compliant and rival contexts. For a reward-trained Gemma-2-9B policy that answers falsely on all evaluated rival trials, the conventional probe scores $0.006 \pm 0.005$ AUROC across three training seeds, while mixed-fit probes score $1.000$ on the same held-out activations. Mixed fitting uses more training examples and access to labelled rival contexts, so this comparison establishes linear recoverability rather than isolating the benefit of decorrelation. We also show that two compliant-fit probes, both perfect in-distribution, score $0.080$ and $0.986$ on the same rival activations. The findings concern what a probe measures: they do not establish preserved functional belief, causal use of the recovered direction, or a deployable deception detector. Code and aggregate results accompany the paper.

</details>


### [60] [Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss: Penalizing Center-Class Hedging in Ordinal Classification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10752)
*Manisha Kandel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出自适应间隔序数损失AMOL，通过乘性权重抑制"中心类对冲"，并引入CHR诊断指标，在四个基准上取得最佳或并列最佳QWK。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准交叉熵损失在序数分类任务中会使网络将预测向中间类靠拢（中心类对冲），因为预测中间类能最小化期望对称损失，成为阻力最小的路径。现有序数损失虽解决大误差惩罚和排序一致性等问题，但均未针对"真实标签相对序数中心的位置"直接抑制中心类对冲。

Method: 提出自适应间隔序数损失（AMOL），对逐类损失项施加乘性权重 m(k,y)=1+α·(1-|k-c|/c)·(|y-c|/c)，其中c为中心类，k为候选类，y为真实标签。该权重编码联合条件：仅当候选类靠近中心且真实标签远离中心时取较大值，其余情况退化为标准行为。引入中心对冲率（CHR）作为直接量化该失效模式的诊断指标。并提出非对称变体AMOL-asym。

Result: 在四个序数分类基准、五个随机种子上，AMOL相比交叉熵、OLL和SORD基线，在全部四个数据集上取得最佳或并列最佳的二次加权Kappa（QWK）。在Abalone数据集上，AMOL-asym完全消除中心类对冲（CHR=0.000±0.000，五个种子一致，每次运行约266个极端类测试样本），而标准交叉熵为0.074±0.005。

Conclusion: AMOL通过对损失项施加依赖真实标签相对中心位置的乘性权重，有效抑制中心类对冲，在多个序数基准上达到最优QWK；AMOL-asym可完全消除该失效模式，验证了方法的有效性。

Abstract: Standard cross-entropy loss causes neural networks trained on ordinal classification tasks to hedge predictions toward center classes, a failure mode we term \emph{center-class hedging}. This occurs because predicting the middle class minimizes expected symmetric loss, making it the path of least resistance regardless of the true label. Existing ordinal losses address related problems such as large-error penalization and rank consistency, but none directly suppresses center-class hedging as a function of where the true label lies relative to the ordinal center. We propose the Adaptive Margin Ordinal Loss (AMOL), a multiplicative weight applied to per-class loss terms of the form $m(k,y) = 1 + α\cdot (1 - |k-c|/c) \cdot (|y-c|/c)$, where $c$ is the center class, $k$ is the candidate class, and $y$ is the true label. The weight encodes a joint condition: it is large only when the candidate class is near center and the true label is far from center, collapsing to standard behavior otherwise. We further introduce the Center-Hedging Rate (CHR) as a diagnostic metric that directly quantifies this failure mode. Across four ordinal classification benchmarks and five random seeds, AMOL achieves the best or tied-best Quadratic Weighted Kappa (QWK) on all four datasets compared to cross-entropy, OLL, and SORD baselines. An asymmetric variant (AMOL-asym) eliminates center-class hedging entirely on the Abalone dataset ($\text{CHR} = 0.000 \pm 0.000$ across all five seeds, $n \approx 266$ extreme-class test samples per run), compared to $0.074 \pm 0.005$ for standard cross-entropy.

</details>


### [61] [A Bellman Optimality Equation for Plasticity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10776)
*Jeremy Lucas,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将持续强化学习中的稳定性-可塑性权衡重新表述为赋能-可塑性权衡，并提出在马尔可夫决策过程中优化可塑性的初步工作，证明存在与赋能类似的贝尔曼最优方程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 持续强化学习中，稳定性与可塑性的权衡是核心难题。Abel等人(2025)将可塑性定义为从观察到动作的广义有向信息，将赋能定义为从动作到观察的广义有向信息，从而将传统权衡重构为赋能-可塑性权衡。然而，尽管赋能优化已有大量研究，在新定义下优化可塑性却尚无研究。

Method: 作者在马尔可夫决策过程框架下开展可塑性优化的初步研究，分析并推导可塑性优化的数学形式，类比此前赋能优化的工作，构建可塑性优化的贝尔曼最优性方程。

Result: 作者证明存在一个用于优化可塑性的贝尔曼最优性方程，其形式与之前赋能优化的工作类似。

Conclusion: 该初步工作为在新定义下优化可塑性提供了理论基础，表明可塑性优化可借助贝尔曼最优性方程来实现，为后续持续强化学习研究开辟了方向。

Abstract: In continual reinforcement learning, carefully managing the stability-plasticity tradeoff remains a core challenge. Recent work by Abel et al. (2025) formalized this dilemma by defining plasticity as the generalized directed information from an agent's observations to its actions, and empowerment as the generalized directed information from its actions to its observations. This formulation successfully reframes the traditional stability-plasticity tradeoff as an empowerment-plasticity tradeoff. However, while extensive literature exists on optimizing for empowerment, there is currently no research addressing the optimization of plasticity under this new definition. This paper presents preliminary work toward optimizing plasticity within Markov decision processes. We show that there exists a Bellman optimality equation for optimizing plasticity similar to previous work for empowerment.

</details>


### [62] [REVA: Reusable Evidence View Aggregation for Context-Efficient RAG Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.11209)
*Tuan Nguyen,Qiran Hu,Banruo Liu,Khoa D. Doan,Kok-Seng Wong,Fan Lai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出REVA框架，从数据挖掘视角将历史查询-文档-模型交互聚合为可复用的证据视图，实现无需辅助模型的RAG后检索压缩，在提升生成质量的同时大幅降低压缩开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG通过检索文档提升知识密集型LLM应用效果，但更长上下文会增加延迟、KV缓存内存和token成本。现有后检索压缩方法多为每个查询独立操作、依赖辅助模型或重写，且引入的在线开销可能抵消缩短提示带来的收益。作者还发现现代压缩器相对简单截断的增益不稳定，并会显著增加推理延迟。

Method: 提出可复用证据视图聚合（REVA）框架：将目标生成器的历史注意力轨迹挖掘为以文档为键、与预算无关的评分存储；将token级注意力映射到可读的词单元；在重复文档访问中聚合重要性；渲染预算特定的纯文本视图，保持文档顺序和标准RAG接口。

Result: 在四个代表性基准和现代LLM上，REVA相比现有先进方法将生成质量提升1.0–5.8个点，同时将压缩开销降低5.3–15.6倍，额外延迟低于40毫秒。

Conclusion: REVA通过复用历史交互数据实现高效、高质量的RAG压缩，兼顾生成质量与推理效率，为RAG后检索压缩提供了新的数据挖掘视角。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) improves knowledge-intensive large language model (LLM) applications by conditioning generation on retrieved documents, but longer contexts increase latency, key-value (KV) cache memory, and token cost. Post-retrieval compression can reduce this cost, yet existing compressors often operate independently for each query, rely on auxiliary models or rewriting, and introduce online overhead that can offset the benefit of shorter prompts. We revisit RAG compression from a data-mining perspective by aggregating historical query--document--model interactions into reusable evidence views. We first show that modern compressors have unstable gains over simple truncation and can add substantial inference-time latency. We then propose Reusable Evidence View Aggregation (REVA), a framework that mines the target generator's historical attention traces into a document-keyed, budget-agnostic score store. REVA maps token-level attention to readable word units, aggregates importance across repeated document accesses, and renders budget-specific plain-text views that preserve document order and the standard RAG interface. Across four representative benchmarks and modern LLMs, REVA improves generation quality by 1.0--5.8 points over existing advances, while reducing compression overhead by a factor of 5.3 to 15.6, adding less than 40 ms of latency.

</details>


### [63] [Counterfactual Marginalisation: Framework for Evaluating Robustness to Nuisance Variables](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10778)
*Yasin Ibrahim,Hermione Warr,Robin J. Evans,Konstantinos Kamnitsas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出反事实边缘化（CF marginalisation）作为测试时评估方法，通过生成反事实图像并平均预测，量化分类模型对人口统计等捷径变量的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习模型可能在测试集表现良好，但实际上依赖人口统计或采集相关的捷径（shortcut）变量，而非真正的因果特征，因此需要评估模型对这类变量的鲁棒性。

Method: 给定一个反事实图像生成器，对年龄、性别等干扰父变量进行干预，为每张测试图像生成反事实版本，并在目标干预分布上对预测取平均，得到干预感知的预测，从而边缘化人口统计效应同时保留患者特异性的潜在信息。基于此定义CF风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感性等指标。

Result: 通过在目标干预分布上平均反事实预测，得到干预感知预测，并据此定义CF风险、校准、稳定性和最坏情况敏感性四类指标，用于定量鲁棒性评估。

Conclusion: 该框架在定量鲁棒性评估方面具有实用价值，可用于检验分类模型对人口统计/采集捷径的依赖性。

Abstract: Machine learning models can achieve strong test performance while relying on demographic or acquisition-related shortcuts. We propose counterfactual (CF) marginalisation as a test-time evaluation procedure for assessing robustness of classification models to such variables. Given a CF image generator, we intervene on nuisance parent variables such as age or sex, generate CF versions of each test image, and average predictions over a target intervention distribution. This produces intervention-aware predictions that marginalise demographic effects while preserving patient-specific latent information. We use these predictions to define metrics for CF risk, calibration, stability and worst-case sensitivity. We demonstrate this framework's utility for quantitative robustness evaluation.

</details>


### [64] [From Connectivity to Rewards: Dense Reward Learning with Directed State Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10781)
*Shuyuan Zhang,Zihan Wang,Xiao-Wen Chang,Doina Precup*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文提出G2QDR框架，通过状态连通性模型预测非对称环境中的成对状态连通强度，并将其转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，以增强图引导的Goal-Conditioned分层强化学习（GCHRL）在稀疏奖励环境下的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有将图与GCHRL结合的方法大多只把图当作随机采样工具，忽略了图内在的连通性信息，未能充分利用底层拓扑结构来高效学习。在准度量（quasimetric）环境中，状态转移固有的非对称性给稳定策略学习和鲁棒路径规划带来根本性挑战，这一问题尤为突出。

Method: 提出状态连通性模型，用于预测非对称环境中成对状态的连通强度；将这些连通强度转化为标量辅助稠密奖励，在多个分层层级上提供连续引导。该状态连通性模型通过一个在探索过程中生成的有向状态图上训练的神经网络高效实现。理论上，G2QDR可集成到任意现有GCHRL架构中。

Result: 在多种稀疏奖励环境上的实证结果表明，G2QDR总体上能够以可接受的计算开销提升基线GCHRL方法的性能。

Conclusion: G2QDR通过挖掘图的连通性信息并将其转化为稠密奖励，有效弥补了现有图引导GCHRL方法在非对称环境中的不足，且具有良好的通用性与较低的计算成本。

Abstract: The integration of graphs with Goal-Conditioned Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning (GCHRL) has received increasing attention, as graphs naturally encode task hierarchies for effective subgoal sampling. However, existing methods often overlook intrinsic connectivity information, failing to fully leverage the underlying topology for efficient learning. Most graph-based GCHRL methods use the graph as a stochastic sampling tool rather than as an environmental model that encodes connectivity and state-accessibility information. This limitation is particularly acute in quasimetric environments, where the inherent asymmetry of state transitions poses a fundamental challenge to stable policy learning and robust path planning. In this paper, we address these problems by introducing a state connectivity model designed to predict pairwise state connectivity strength in asymmetric environments. We transform these connectivity strengths into scalar auxiliary dense rewards, providing continuous guidance across multiple hierarchical levels. We demonstrate that our proposed framework, Graph-Guided Quasimetric Dense Reward (G2QDR), can theoretically be integrated into any existing GCHRL architecture, and the state connectivity model is efficiently implemented via a neural network trained on a directed state graph generated during exploration. Empirical results across a wide range of sparse reward environments indicate that, in general, G2QDR can enhance the performance of baseline GCHRL approaches with acceptable computational overhead.

</details>


### [65] [DR-LabStack: Design and Implementation of a Clinician-Facing Web System for Diabetic Retinopathy Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10796)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该论文设计并实现 DR-LabStack（React-Flask 网页系统），将四个异构的预训练糖尿病视网膜病变模型统一集成到同一临床界面，并对其软件功能进行了验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有的糖尿病视网膜病变预训练模型在输入字段、序列化格式、预处理要求和输出语义上各不相同，难以通过统一的临床界面访问，因此需要用户界面与推理服务之间的显式协调。

Method: 采用 React-Flask 架构，集成 RuleFit、Pruned RuleFit、Elaborative XGBoost 和 Two-level Ensemble 四个外部模型。共享表单获取有序特征并渲染数值与分类控件，构建位置输入向量；后端适配器加载异构产物并应用集成模型的配套缩放器；统一 JSON 响应支持二分类展示及方法与来源信息。

Result: 在隔离环境中使用真实模型产物评估：四个模型均成功加载并暴露 14、6、8、25 字段契约；62 次 Flask 测试客户端请求刻画服务行为，12 次受限向量检查确认调用路径与阈值一致性；24 个浏览器组件场景（模拟传输）验证输入排序、结果渲染并刻画输入校验行为。

Conclusion: 系统为异构糖尿病视网膜病变模型提供了可复用的交互与服务化工作流；贡献在于网页系统设计、集成与软件功能，临床有效性与临床医生可用性仍需单独评估。

Abstract: Pretrained diabetic retinopathy (DR) prediction models differ in their input fields, serialization formats, preprocessing requirements, and output semantics. Making these models accessible through a common clinical interface therefore requires explicit coordination between the user interface and the inference service. We designed and implemented DR-LabStack, a React-Flask web system integrating four externally developed pretrained models: RuleFit, Pruned RuleFit, Elaborative XGBoost, and Two-level Ensemble. A shared form retrieves ordered model features, renders model-specific numerical and categorical controls, and constructs a positional input vector. Backend adapters load heterogeneous artifacts and apply the ensemble's accompanying scaler, while a common JSON response supports binary classification display alongside method and source information. Functional evaluation on September 8, 2026 used copied application files and real model artifacts in a documented isolated environment. All four models loaded and exposed their 14-, 6-, 8-, and 25-field contracts. Sixty-two Flask test-client requests characterized service behavior; 12 limited-vector checks confirmed invocation-path and threshold consistency. Twenty-four browser-component scenarios with mocked transport verified input ordering and result rendering and characterized input-validation behavior. The resulting system demonstrates a reusable interaction and serving workflow for heterogeneous DR models. The contribution is web-system design, integration, and software functionality; clinical effectiveness and clinician usability require separate evaluation.

</details>


### [66] [RiVaT-Fuse: Reliability-Calibrated Variational Tensor Fusion for Multimodal Prediction under Modality Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10798)
*Yingfan Xu,Tieming Liu,Ye Liang,Taiping Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出RiVaT-Fuse，将图像-元数据融合重新定义为样本级隐状态估计，用可靠性校准的变分张量融合替代传统聚合式融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有表示级融合方法（拼接、门控、条件调制、注意力）只选择聚合架构，未明确定义在模态不确定性下"融合表示"应具有何种含义，且证据可靠性随样本和潜在因子变化。

Method: 以变分目标估计共识隐状态，平衡图像证据、元数据证据、结构化跨模态交互与稳定性；用矩阵值信任几何替代标量模态置信度；将交互分解为加性、乘性和关系性三部分；耦合隐状态与条件鲁棒性及结构化多任务预测；给出隐式求解的适定性与稳定性解释，并用高效的"低秩+对角"信任算子实例化。

Result: 在图像级图像-元数据预测基准上，RiVaT-Fuse在直接表示级基线中取得最佳整体预测排名，同时在扰动下提升了概率与标签稳定性。

Conclusion: 将融合建模为样本级隐状态估计并在变分框架中显式刻画可靠性几何，可同时提升预测性能与鲁棒性。

Abstract: Image-metadata prediction requires fusing heterogeneous evidence whose reliability can vary across samples and latent factors. Existing representation-level fusion methods typically choose an aggregation architecture, such as concatenation, gating, conditional modulation, or attention, without explicitly defining what the fused representation should mean under modality uncertainty. We propose RiVaT-Fuse, a reliability-calibrated variational tensor fusion framework that defines fusion as sample-wise latent-state estimation. Rather than producing a fused vector by direct aggregation, RiVaT-Fuse estimates a consensus latent state through a variational objective that balances image evidence, metadata evidence, structured cross-modal interaction, and stability. The resulting framework replaces scalar modality confidence with matrix-valued trust geometry, decomposes interaction into additive, multiplicative, and relational components, and couples the latent state with conditional robustness and structured multi-task prediction. We provide well-posedness and stability interpretations of the latent solve and instantiate the framework with efficient low-rank-plus-diagonal trust operators. On an image-level image-metadata prediction benchmark, RiVaT-Fuse achieves the strongest overall predictive rank among direct representation-level baselines while improving probability and label stability under perturbation.

</details>


### [67] [Processing and classifying bird songs using wavelet techniques and supervised learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.10826)
*Laura Lucia Dominguez Barrios,Fidel Aniano Causil Barrios,Alex Rodrigo dos Santos Sousa,Mariana Rodrigues Motta*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个针对高噪声自然声景中入侵鸟类鸣声的去噪与分类一体化框架，利用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法处理信号，并对比多种监督学习模型，SVM在10维MFCC下达到最高准确率0.9398。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自然声景中环境噪声高，导致入侵鸟类鸣声信号退化，影响自动化生态监测与生物入侵管理中的识别效果，因此需要有效的去噪与分类方法。

Method: 采用基于Epanechnikov核先验的贝叶斯小波收缩方法进行信号去噪，具有闭式决策规则和较高计算效率；对iNaturalist平台上三种鸟类录音处理后，提取MFCC、熵、过零率等特征，并评估Random Forest、Multinomial Logistic Regression和SVM在不同特征维度下的表现。

Result: 基于小波预处理显著提升了分类性能，SVM在10维MFCC配置下取得最高准确率（最高0.9398）。

Conclusion: 该研究为自动化生态监测和生物入侵管理提供了一种稳健的统计工具。

Abstract: This study proposes an integrated framework for the processing and classification of invasive bird species vocalizations within natural soundscapes, characterized by high levels of environmental noise.
